absolutely isn t   i didn t know it was a thing the game had  and the enemy health and the damage i took felt so utterly random that it drove me to openly investigate   landing headshots constantly  6 rounds to the head just to stumble   firing like an idiot before engaging them  same zombie took 1 connected rounds to the chest for the same effect  lo and behold  forced adaptive difficulty   it was meant to enforce a style of play  a sort of hit and run thing  however  to stop skilled players from blazing through the game  too quickly   they introduced arbitrary nerfs and damage sponging that only worsen in scale to how much better you are doing   the basics of it are that if you do poorly  miss  take damage  etc   you will gain a handicap to help you   this is the good part about the difficulty that s great for players new to the franchise or type of game  it can boost your damage  get you more ammo at nodes  and have you suffer less damage on attacks  it s a layer of forgiveness that will  wear off  as the player gains skill   the bad part is that if you do well  it is a pendulum  not a buffer   the swing overcompensates in the opposite direction  in all the wrong ways for a survival horror   you will find less ammo at the already limited nodes   i craft less ammo from resources  i   that paltry ammo you just discovered  i will do less damage  i    this means that to get back out of that room you planned that run to  you have to spend  i all  i  that ammo you just found  plus five extra rounds  just to stun and run  top this off with the longer you go without getting damaged  the more damage you take on the next hit  letting even a basic zombie put you in a last hit danger state in a single landed attack   the zombies inherently have an ability where if they grab you from behind  it s like a backstab and deals way more damage  forcing you to watch your back a lot more   that is not what i am referring to  trying to tank a hit after you ve done well enough because you don t want to waste a sub weapon to counter the zombie  just means you re going to need to use a health item instead because of how much damage it does   it s worsened by the fact that once you understand how it works  the difficulty levels are just a handwave   they lock you to a certain  intensity  of this adaptive difficulty   assisted locks it to the lower tiers  hardcore locks it to the highest tiers  and the standard difficulty is wildly inconsistent   the reason why  it is not limited to tiers like the other two  you can descend into assisted level bonuses  or  advance  to what is literally just enemies and supply problems from hardcore mode  only you don t qualify for hardcore mode achievements because  you re playing on standard    the worst part is they added critical hits   you can  and will  randomly get head popping criticals that make sure the enemy will stay dead    but why bad     they re random   a zombie can take 18 rounds to the face and only fall over  meaning they can get up later  or you ll stop the same zombie after 2 hits to the chest on a different save and they ll never wake up again   this is a game about resources   seeing that crit happen on the last round in the mag isn t a relief  it s seeing that the other 23 rounds to the head didn t actually matter  and realizing your conservation of ammunition   a staple of the gameplay  has been reduced to a collectathon formality because at the difficulty you ve been  rewarded  with  only the crits matter  even worse  it can feel  i organized   i   like the entire fight with x or y resident evil 2 remake is the most cohesive game i ve ever played  and honestly one of the best designed games i ve played in general   the map and mission design are on another level  i don t just mean  good for a horror game  either  i truly mean the kind of level design that developers across the industry should be studying  every room  shortcut  key item  and puzzle feels carefully placed  and the game constantly has you going back through familiar areas without it ever feeling repetitive  the r p d  is easily one of the best maps ever made in gaming  what impressed me even more was how immersive the world felt  having most of the game take place on one connected map with seamless transitions between areas made everything feel real  instead of feeling like a bunch of separate levels  it actually feels like one place you re slowly learning and navigating over time  very few single player games pull that off as well as this   the story was another surprise  zombie stories are usually pretty predictable and all follow the same kind of formula  but resident evil 2 remake actually feels grounded while still being creative  the characters feel like real people stuck in a disaster  and the umbrella conspiracy  the g virus  and everything going on under raccoon city gives it its own identity compared to most zombie media   i also liked that the zombies were a bit tanky  in a lot of games that would be annoying  but here it makes sense  leon is a rookie cop walking into a nightmare  not some overpowered action hero  every encounter feels dangerous  and deciding whether to spend ammo or try to slip past enemies actually matters  mr  x also adds a nice bit of spice to the game  whenever you hear those footsteps  you immediately start thinking about how you re going to get around him since he can cut off a big chunk of the map  he forces you to adapt on the fly and keeps the tension high without ever feeling annoying   claire redfield is also a big part of what makes the game work  unlike leon  she isn t a cop or trained fighter—just an ordinary person looking for her brother who ends up trapped in the outbreak  this gives her campaign a different tone  framing her more as a real survivor character who relies on improvisation and quick thinking rather than training  a lot of her moments also come from trying to protect sherry  which adds emotional weight on top of the horror  claire represents the human side of the disaster  focused on survival and helping someone else while everything falls apart   hunk is an absolute gigachad  the man gets dropped into a universe sized nightmare filled with more b o w s in minutes than most re protagonists deal with in an entire campaign  loses his squad  and still treats escaping raccoon city like it s just another day at work  i ve actually replayed the game more just to play his mode because it s that fun  his theme song is also peak  he just reminds me of those strong silent type protagonists from early 2000s games that you don t really see much anymore   resident evil 2 remake is one of those rare games where everything just clicks  the atmosphere  level design  survival mechanics  story  and replayability all work together perfectly  if someone asked me what the greatest survival horror is  i would point to this remake   ex re hater  current re truther 9 7 10

after completing all 44 achievements  multiple s  runs  challenge runs  and every extra mode  i can confidently say that resident evil 2 remake is an outstanding modernization of the classic formula   what impressed me the most is how the game creates tension without relying on outdated mechanics  the difficulty comes from decision making  route optimization  inventory management  and resource conservation—not from fighting against the controls or awkward camera angles   mr  x deserves special praise  he wasn t scary to me as much as he was stressful in the best possible way  his presence constantly forces you to adapt your plans  rethink your routes  and manage your resources more carefully  even after several playthroughs  hearing his footsteps nearby never stops creating pressure   the map design is excellent  once you learn the rpd and start optimizing your routes  each run becomes faster and more satisfying  going from feeling lost to completing s  runs in under two hours is incredibly rewarding   the extra modes are also fantastic  hunk and tofu add a fun twist to the gameplay  while  the ghost survivors  offers unique challenges that test your knowledge of the mechanics in different ways   no time to mourn  was definitely the toughest challenge for me  but overcoming it felt great   if i had one minor criticism  it s that completing s  on the b scenarios doesn t unlock any additional rewards or achievements  considering the effort required  some extra rewards would have been nice   overall  resident evil 2 remake perfectly balances survival horror  action  and strategic gameplay  it respects the original while modernizing everything that needed to evolve   whether you re a longtime fan or completely new to the series  this is an easy recommendation 9 10

is it a perfect remake  no  is it a good one  yes    b  pros   b   one of the remake s greatest strengths is its atmosphere  the game is dark  unsettling  and constantly keeps players on edge  every hallway  room  and corner feels threatening  creating a sense of tension that rarely lets up  the sound design and lighting work together exceptionally well to make exploration both exciting and nerve racking   the graphics are another major highlight  capcom did an outstanding job with the visuals  delivering detailed environments and impressive character models  the raccoon city police department  sewers  and laboratory all look fantastic   the gameplay is also excellent  the over the shoulder camera perspective is a perfect fit for a modern resident evil title  striking a strong balance between action and survival horror  combat feels satisfying  resource management remains important  and the controls are responsive and intuitive  the remake successfully modernizes the classic gameplay without losing the tension and challenge that made the original game memorable    b  cons   b   while the story is enjoyable  it feels like a missed opportunity in some areas  the game could have benefited from additional cutscenes  particularly regarding william birkin s transformation  in the original game  we were given a much longer  and frankly badass  cutscene showing birkin s decision to inject himself with the g virus and then the massacre of the umbrella soldiers  in the remake  much of this is reduced to a brief video recording found during the campaign  making his transformation feel less impactful and less emotionally engaging   another disappointment is the limited interaction between claire and leon  i almost forgot the other character even existed halfway through my first playthrough back in 2019  since both characters are experiencing the same outbreak from different perspectives  it would have been nice to see more scenes that develop their relationship and show them working together throughout the story  in the original game  they interacted in the s t a r s  office and regularly communicated via radio during certain sections  making it feel as though the other half of the story was still unfolding even when we weren t with them  their interactions are memorable  but there simply are not enough of them   spoiler  despite the two notes left by the other character in the second run and the brief scene before the final boss fight    spoiler   the biggest issue is the implementation of the second run  aside from a few alterations and an  spoiler  additional final boss battle   spoiler   it feels largely unnecessary  many of the same puzzles  boss fights  and locations are reused with only minor changes  furthermore  there are several inconsistencies between scenario a and scenario b  creating confusion regarding the timeline and events  rather than feeling like two complementary campaigns that tell different sides of the same story  the second run often comes across as a simplified copy and paste version of the first scenario  a more distinct and divergent approach would have made replaying the game far more rewarding    b  overall   b  re2r is an outstanding survival horror game that successfully modernizes a beloved classic  its atmospheric world  impressive graphics  and excellent gameplay make it one of the strongest entries in the series  however  the story could have explored certain characters and events in greater depth  and the second run system falls short of the dual scenario concept established by the original game  despite these shortcomings  it remains a fantastic remake and a must play for both longtime fans and newcomers to the franchise

the resident evil 2 remake is a true survival horror game in every sense of the word  in the traditional sense  which means that even on the easiest difficulty  the gameplay is balanced enough that you can go on a heroic shooting spree  try to wipe out the entire zombie population  and then  after a possible positive outcome  receive the title of “benefactor of the city” from the mayor   but even here  the line is thin  and if you don t pay close enough attention  you can easily come out on the losing end  on harder difficulty levels  however  you really have to pay attention  your inventory is limited and can only be expanded with additional bags  gradually  in small steps   you have to strategize about what to bring with you   it s best to ditch all less important items and carry just one or two weapons   and you have so little ammo that you can t stop to spar with every single zombie that comes along  from this point on  the focus is truly on survival  it s only worth engaging in combat with those who are specifically blocking your path  and even larger enemy types should be avoided   unless you re facing a boss  in such cases  looting every safe  portable storage unit  and locker is essential and unavoidable  and you must also craft the best tools from raw materials  otherwise you might run out of ammunition and medicine  by the way  it s also worth gathering up any weapon accessories you can find  otherwise  it ll take a very long time to take down even the most basic monsters   of course  the initial playthrough  with a single character  takes roughly 8–10 hours  but it can be extended and replayed   which is highly recommended   and you ll always receive a score for your performance  which can then improve your chances on subsequent playthroughs  so there s plenty of content  though the focus is still on the visuals  atmosphere  and storytelling   for newcomers  the new re 2 is a fresh experience right from the start  familiarity with the original or its predecessors isn t necessary  that said  i should add that for those who are used to modern games  the gameplay mechanics   which are still rooted in the classic installments   might feel slower  more complicated  and clunkier  even with the streamlining  for this reason  i recommend that newcomers let go a little of the constant barrage of stimulations coming from all directions  put themselves in the right mindset  and imagine they re at the police station  where their only chance of survival is to adapt to the situation at hand   one in which neither the internet nor a million companions and sources of help are available  otherwise  veterans will feel right at home despite the changes  because while the game boasts fantastic visuals thanks to the re engine   which was already successfully deployed in resident evil 7   and its physics are generally very solid  the fundamentals remain familiar   but i ll go a step further and say that no one who s already played through the classic adventure will feel like anything is missing  the story is different here and there  streamlined  and to a greater or lesser extent  even the locations have been tweaked so that the return of re2 doesn t just feel like a rip off  surprises come almost constantly  the comfort zone  as such  completely disappears for veterans  and this kind of disruption works perfectly not only with the incredibly intense suspense but also in terms of gore  resident evil 2 is downright brutal  limbs are torn off  guts spill out  and we re wading through blood and gore  which might turn the stomachs of the less hardened in some places  capcom s remake is no walk in the park  but it s an unforgettable experience for everyone   that much is certain    going back to what i wrote earlier  yes  we gave up years ago on the idea that capcom would ever do anything serious with the resident evil series  and here we are  the company didn t drag their feet on remastering the second episode because they weren t paying attention to it  but because they clearly knew that this is perhaps the most important installment in the series   one that can t  or rather shouldn t  just be thrown together and tossed to fans with a “here you go  take it  be happy with it ” no  here you can feel the care  the love for one of the greatest legends of survival horror   without which many other classics might never have been born   while in terms of gameplay and narrative structure  they managed to create something that remains virtually unmatched to this day  and essentially  we ve been given a completely new game that shares the same roots  with level design that s still brilliant and almost on par with dark souls  but it offers so many differences   especially when considering the visuals and other elements   that there s no question  this is definitely worth the full price  as this isn t a remaster  but a truly 2019 game that s modern in almost every single aspect   it s interesting  but it can be said here and there that the original version owes its success in part to its unique and still memorable melodies  none of which are present here   though that s not entirely true  since we can choose the original ost  it just hasn t been modernized  essentially  this is one of  or perhaps the only real flaw in the new resident evil  otherwise  i d only be nitpicking  leon and claire s adventure is still brilliant and thrilling  the characters are still well developed  the atmosphere is incredible  the visuals are just as good  and i even flinched a few times myself  so congratulations to the publisher and the developers  and i d like to express my sincere thanks for the experience  i recommend it wholeheartedly  it s a must play for fans of the genre and the series  a legendary remake of a legendary title

there s about a dozen different ways resident evil 2 could have gone wrong  the hit or miss company that capcom is remaking one of the most classic re games in the style of third person action adventure games that have bloated the industry since resident evil 4  this struggle to keep the original s gameplay and map design in tact on top of crafting a narrative that takes itself quite seriously  with far improved cinematography and acting performances  it s a recipe for an underwhelming experience that leaves you wondering why it exists in the first place  resident evil 2 remake is a welcome exception   i ve never played the original  but the tight level design and the meticulous encounters across each room are continuously thrilling  and the game did well with not falling into the trap that the new perspective could have set for it  combat is still incredibly suspenseful  not only for the presentation  but for the constant dread of losing essential ammo hanging over your head  to give me this feeling for both of my playthroughs shows just how much care was taken to keep players on edge  unless you hear that save room music  you re always a corner away from a unique encounter that will test your resource management    it s no wonder that  with how much a normal enemy makes the player think and fear  that the many encounters with the tyrant are as thrilling as they are  the giant stalker enemy excels in both the sound design of his boots stomping across the floor and his hollow  null expression  backtracking is made infinitely more thrilling with his presence as he roams the hub world  not only does the character succeed in his own right  but he is an excellent reminder of how disadvantaged the player has always been   he is never going away  and he is never going to stop  and any situation you find yourself in going forward can go from manageable to lethal in an instant  all it takes is hearing those boots in an adjacent room  stomping closer and closer  it s just great  the survival aspect is great  but its intensity may lead to some frustrating moments  as the horror of certain scenarios is overshadowed by the annoyance of a particular zombie that is taking a firing squad to the face over minutes  but continues to stand up  or your player character uttering voice lines out of your control when standing inches away from an enemy that can only track you with hearing   these were exceptions  but my playthrough was a few days longer due to setting the game aside because of these uncommon  but frustrating moments that just killed any immersion or momentum i had going in an area  skill issue  perhaps   the story of the original game has been faithfully adapted and revamped  giving the incredibly cheesy plots that the series is known for some well deserved and earned dramatic moments with the remake s take on the plot  it s nothing ground breaking  as resident evil has never been known for its subtlety  but the mix found between the game s more serious tone and remaining loyal to the inherent goofiness that is the franchise is commendable   adapted faithfully as well are the two routes  with both choices offering a different playable character  weapons unique to them  and their unique view on the events of the story  i mostly enjoyed seeing claire s perspective after she is separated from leon  but the canonicity of the route left me confused more often than not  there are many instances where claire s route changes from leon  being given more access to parts of the hub world leon could previously not access or certain narrative moments that are altered in minor ways to accommodate leon s presence elsewhere in racoon city  such as the tyrant not chasing claire in some key moments where its clear to an observant player that he would be busy stalking leon  but at the same time  claire fights multiple bosses throughout her route that contradict the flow of events in leon s story   that s not to say this was a substantial issue  but claire s route  my second playthrough  was far more compelling when i was seeing a whole new side of the narrative or fighting a boss that leon never even saw a hint of in his route  these are somewhat of a rarity in claire s route  and the time spent in her playthrough that felt like repetition was noticeable  especially around the midpoint  by the time of the true ending  i was left satisfied with claire s route  as her final hours of playtime finished strong narratively and gameplay wise  with my favorite set piece and solid development conclusion for a character i feel sorely needed it during leon s route   resident evil 2 was exactly what i expected  and i thoroughly enjoyed it  it s a landmark achievement of the survival horror genre brought to an entirely new generation of players  my preference with the series will always lie in the more action packed entries such as 4 and 5  but this classic has aged very well in a modern format  it s success is measured in many ways  but i feel one that goes understated is how easily players of the original game and this remake discuss their preferred games    when a legendary original game and a remake that had to grow in its shadow during the entire development cycle can coexist so easily and bring the community veterans and newcomers together over the shared love of what is a fundamentally identical  but very different game  that is a remake that has more than earned the right to exist

 b resident evil 2  2019   b  successfully reimagines the 1998 classic as modern survival horror  pairing responsive over the shoulder combat with excellent atmosphere  careful resource management  and one of the strongest versions of the raccoon city police department   i played it at launch and recently returned to it on linux ahead of  i resident evil requiem  i   completing leon s standard campaign and claire s hardcore 2nd run   the moment to moment modernization still works extremely well   combat is smoother without becoming disposable  ammunition remains valuable  zombies can absorb enough punishment to make avoiding a fight worthwhile  and tightening the aiming reticle rewards patience rather than constant firing  exploration remains just as important  with puzzles  inventory management  weapon upgrades  hip pouches  and discardable key items keeping the traditional survival horror loop intact   mr  x may be the reimagining s best addition  his dynamic patrols turn familiar hallways into constantly changing routes  forcing you to reconsider where safety actually exists   where the game becomes more complicated is in how it reinterprets the structure of the 1998 original    h2 the good  h2   list      b atmosphere  b   exceptional lighting  dense shadows  environmental detail  and directional audio make the rpd consistently oppressive      b combat  b   responsive shooting and deliberate resource management successfully modernize the original survival horror formula      b exploration  b   puzzles  inventory management  weapon parts  hip pouches  and the redesigned map make searching the environment consistently rewarding      b mr  x  b   dynamic patrols force you to abandon comfortable routes and make decisions under pressure      b sound design  b   distant footsteps  groans  environmental noise  and mr  x s unmistakable movement constantly communicate threats you cannot yet see      b voice acting  b   the japanese cast balances grounded performances with the familiar melodrama of resident evil      b extra modes  b   the 4th survivor and ghost survivors provide worthwhile challenges beyond the main campaigns      b linux performance  b   the dx11 non rt branch remained above 200 fps at 1440p with roughly 189 fps 1  lows and no crashes or graphical issues    list    h2 the bad  h2   list      b scenario structure  b   2nd run rearranges items and puzzles but never recreates the interconnected a b scenarios of the 1998 original      b reduced variety  b   missing enemies and other streamlined elements make repeat playthroughs less varied than the original      b sewers  b   the expanded sewer section slows the campaign and becomes more noticeable during repeat runs      b game rank  b   adaptive difficulty changes enemy aggression and resilience behind the scenes  making weapon effectiveness less mechanically consistent      b dx12 stability  b   the default directx 12 renderer caused excessive vram usage and eventual freezes on my linux setup    list    h2 summary  h2   b resident evil 2  2019   b  is an excellent modern survival horror game   its atmosphere remains exceptional  the rpd is beautifully reconstructed  combat balances responsiveness with resource pressure  and mr  x turns exploration into a tense exercise in improvised route planning   the problem is not the modernization itself   it is what happened to one of resident evil 2 s defining structural ideas   the original a b system presented leon and claire as two people surviving the same outbreak from interconnected perspectives  decisions and events could carry between scenarios  making the second campaign feel like another side of the same night   2nd run provides useful gameplay variation through different item placements  altered puzzle solutions  and the true ending  but it does not preserve that relationship  leon and claire repeat major encounters  defeat many of the same bosses  and experience overlapping events that cannot all cleanly coexist   that makes the reimagining feel more focused  but also less ambitious than its source material   the same tension appears elsewhere  the modern combat and exploration are excellent  but reduced enemy variety and the slower sewer section make repeated campaigns less dynamic  game rank maintains pressure effectively  but its hidden adjustments can make enemy resilience harder to read for players who prefer fixed mechanical rules   i also cannot ignore the benchmark established by the 2002  i resident evil  i  remake  that game demonstrated how dramatically a classic could be rebuilt while still expanding its locations  enemies  mechanics  and narrative around the original foundation   resident evil 2 takes a different approach  it successfully translates the core survival horror experience into the re engine  but some of the original s structural ambition is lost along the way   even with those compromises  it remains a strong survival horror reimagining and one of capcom s better modern interpretations of the series    b score  7 5   10  b   b recommended  b

 h1 ah  raccoon city   h1  the game that truly pushed resident evil into the spotlight   while the first game was a masterpiece in its own right and laid the foundation for everything that followed  resident evil 2 is where the franchise really found its identity  the way it tells its story through multiple perspectives and routes was groundbreaking at the time  you step into the shoes of either leon  the rookie cop on his very first day  or claire redfield  the determined sister of chris  two characters  two journeys  one nightmare of a city   the remake absolutely did wonders  using the re engine  capcom brought this familiar story to a whole new audience with a modern touch while still respecting its roots  for those who played the original on the playstation  this feels like a warm but unsettling homecoming  that said  it s not a one to one remake  some elements from the original are missing  but new ideas and refinements were added on top  making it feel fresh rather than copied   one of the strengths of re games is how they reward replayability  the more you play  the more confident you become  you learn shortcuts  optimize routes  shave off minutes in speedruns  and slowly grow bolder when tackling hardcore difficulties  re2 shines here  leon and claire already offer two distinct runs  but with the a and b scenarios  that number doubles  four different playthroughs  each with meaningful variations  and that s before factoring in difficulty modes   the game stays true to survival horror  this isn t a full blown shooter like later entries such as re5 or re6  combat feels refined  especially when targeting limbs and managing crowds  but it never lets you forget that resources are limited  inventory management matters  every bullet  herb  and key item forces you to think ahead  it keeps you tense  cautious  and constantly planning for what might come next   visually and technically  the game is an absolute feast  the re engine is pushed to its limits  delivering an atmosphere that screams resident evil  the environments drip with dread  the lighting is phenomenal  and the character models are incredibly detailed  the enemies are especially impressive and  at times  downright disturbing  william birkin  in particular  is a grotesque work of art that perfectly embodies horror   during my first runs  i was constantly on edge  h1  great modernisation of classic survival horror   h1    resident evil 2 is a fun  tense adaptation of classic resident evil gameplay to modern graphics and controls  as either a fresh faced  pre re4 leon  who still climbs down ladders like a normal human  or as claire redfield  sister of the boulder puncher   you have to survive and escape the zombie virus outbreak of raccoon city  most of the game takes place in a labyrinthine police station  full of rooms to clear and puzzles to solve   as in older survival horror games  resource management is a constant concern  you will have to make every shot count and be ready to avoid enemies if necessary  zombies are tough and will often get back up after you gun them down  which can sometimes be a bit frustrating  but does preserve the economic focus of the genre  skilful movement and aiming will help you only so much  planning and clever decision making are more important to your success   the game is tense  but not massively scary and there are few genuinely creepy moments  in part due to the somewhat limited enemy variety  the puzzles are a lot of fun and at times quite challenging and there are plenty of optional items to find if you re thorough   one playthrough will last you between 6 and 9 hours  but there are two story modes with divergent content and stories   while i wouldn t say that the game is an absolute masterpiece  it is still great fun and well worth playing  i m glad i bought it  but i m also glad i got it deeply discounted

resident evil 2  2019  review  a masterful reimagining that delivers unforgettable tension  resident evil 2  2019  is the ultimate proof that a remake can not only stay true to its roots but also surpass its predecessor in almost every way  capcom s reimagining of the 1998 survival horror classic brings the horrors of raccoon city to life in breathtaking detail  delivering a visually stunning  emotionally charged  and absolutely nerve wracking experience  this game is an instant classic  successfully blending nostalgia with innovation and giving both old fans and newcomers an unforgettable ride   visually stunning  a true showcase of what s possible in modern gaming  the first thing that strikes you about resident evil 2  2019  is just how beautiful it looks  the game runs on the re engine  capcom s proprietary engine  and the result is nothing short of spectacular  from the detailed  atmospheric lighting in the dark corridors of the raccoon city police station to the grotesque  terrifying zombie models  everything feels meticulously crafted  the gore and decay in the environment are believable and visceral  drawing you into a world that feels real yet horrifyingly unnatural   the lighting and shadow effects are particularly impressive  enhancing the horror experience  the way the flashlight illuminates dark rooms  casting ominous shadows  creates an atmosphere of constant tension  keeping you on edge at all times  every inch of the police station feels alive  from the dilapidated offices to the chilling sound of distant moans echoing through the hallways   a compelling  gripping story  familiar yet fresh  at the heart of resident evil 2  2019  is the compelling story that has stood the test of time  players take on the roles of rookie cop leon s  kennedy and college student claire redfield as they try to survive a zombie apocalypse that has overtaken raccoon city  while the plot remains largely the same as the original  the reimagined version breathes new life into the characters and their interactions  making the story feel both nostalgic and fresh   leon is still the naive  determined rookie  while claire is the fierce and resourceful survivor  but their performances feel more nuanced and emotional thanks to excellent voice acting and motion capture  the game expands on their personal stories and relationships  particularly with the addition of their respective side characters—ada wong and sherry birkin—adding depth to the narrative without overshadowing the game s horror elements  these emotional beats create a connection to the characters that makes their struggles feel all the more real   the pacing of the story is expertly balanced  keeping the player invested from beginning to end  you ll constantly uncover new details about the outbreak  the sinister umbrella corporation  and the tragic events unfolding in raccoon city  each new twist and turn adds to the mounting sense of dread   nerve wracking gameplay  every moment feels like a battle for survival  resident evil 2  2019  isn t just a visual and narrative triumph—it s also a masterclass in tense  nerve wracking gameplay  the game retains the fixed camera angles of the original  but with a modern  over the shoulder perspective that enhances both the horror and the action  every corner you turn could hide an ambush  and every encounter feels like a fight for survival   the resource management and combat are what really make this remake shine  ammo is scarce  health items are limited  and the tension never lets up  you ll need to think carefully about how to use your resources  often deciding between taking on enemies head on or finding an alternate route to avoid them  the puzzles  while not overly complex  fit perfectly into the game s atmosphere  and solving them gives you a satisfying sense of progress   but what really heightens the tension is the looming presence of the tyrant  mr  x  his sudden appearance in the police station is a game changer  turning what was previously a relatively contained experience into a nerve shredding game of cat and mouse  the terrifying sound of his heavy footsteps echoing through the halls  combined with the fear that you never know when he ll appear  makes for some of the most intense moments in the game  this cat and mouse dynamic  especially in the latter half  gives the game an edge that keeps the stakes high throughout   a perfect balance of horror and action  what s remarkable about resident evil 2  2019  is how it manages to balance the slow  deliberate tension of survival horror with more intense action moments  the combat is not as frantic as in later entries like resident evil 4 or resident evil 5  but it s far more grounded  every shot counts  every zombie encounter feels consequential  and when you face off against the grotesque mutations of the virus  you re left with a sense of dread and awe   the game s difficulty levels offer a variety of challenges  ensuring that even hardcore fans of the series can find a level that keeps them on the edge of their seats  the classic ‘hardcore  mode  in particular  will test your nerves and survival skills  with enemies that are even more aggressive and fewer resources to rely on   sound and immersion  the final ingredient  the sound design in resident evil 2  2019  is critical to the game s atmosphere  the muffled groans and snarls of zombies  the distant sound of mr  x stomping around  and the creaking of the building all combine to create an unnerving sense of isolation and fear  the lack of a traditional soundtrack in some sections only adds to the tension  leaving you to wonder what s around the next corner  every sound is purposeful  and the silence only serves to make each noise more impactful   final verdict  resident evil 2  2019  is a spectacular remake that sets a new standard for how classic games should be reimagined  it respects the legacy of the original while breathing new life into every corner of raccoon city  with stunning visuals  a compelling narrative  and gameplay that will keep you up at night  the heart pounding tension and unforgettable moments ensure that this game isn t just a one time experience—it s something you ll want to revisit over and over again   for anyone who appreciates survival horror at its finest  resident evil 2  2019  is a must play  it s a perfect blend of nostalgia and innovation  and it s a game that will go down as one of the best remakes ever made   rating  9 5 10
a solid remake of a solid game on 2025   the story  the chance to play both characters  and the variety of puzzles and enemies  design make it a top game for today standards  the graphics remain solid  although a bit washed out   and the camera and combats are satisfying   the only downside is that you may end up running around a bit more than necessary to understand and solve some puzzles  additionally  nowadays the genre is not so revolutionary  and the story is not as impacting emotionally as other games  nevertheless  it is still a very good game   worth playing  whether you played it long ago or not

whether you re a nostalgic fan of the original  or just a fan of zombie games in general  or even just a fan of puzzles and escape rooms  resident evil 2  remake  is a blast  from the halls of the mansion like rpd  to the chaos ridden streets  to the grimy sewers  and the futuristic lab  each part of the game has its own distinct feel  and its own distinct obstacles and enemies to face   the controls are pretty easy  and the enemies have somewhat predictable patterns  but i still found myself being surprised at some of the jump scares and twists that the game pulls on you when you re not alert  overall  where this game thrives is in its atmosphere  specifically with the classic soundtrack turned on   the music is perfect and fits the mood of each area  the flickering lights  the ac noises  the shuffling of the zombies where it s unclear how close or far they are    it all adds up and makes a beautifully haunting experience   the cutscenes and animations are very well done  and almost never break immersion  except for some of the zombie death ragdolls  though they can be kinda funny   the voice acting is really good  especially from sherry  the little girl  her fear is palpable and her emotional moments really got me tearing up  claire is also a good voice actor  even though sometimes she doesn t seem that scared ig  her determination and her quick thinking is badass  though  i wish she seemed a bit more tense given the situation   all in all  great game  i d give it an easy 8 or 9 out of 10  and would definitely say it s worth the money  especially on sale 
resident evil 2 remake  a thrilling rebirth of survival horror  the resident evil 2 remake is a stunning reimagining of a beloved classic that brilliantly fuses modern technology with the atmospheric terror of its origins  from the moment you step into the rain soaked streets of raccoon city  a sense of impending dread pervades every shadow and sound  the remake not only revitalizes the game s visuals and controls but also deepens the narrative  making it as emotionally resonant as it is delightfully terrifying   gameplay and mechanics the gameplay feels both familiar and innovative  striking a perfect balance between methodical resource management and heart pounding action  with its enhanced over the shoulder perspective  encounters with grotesque creatures and the relentless pursuit of survival become detailed and immersive  the controls have been meticulously refined  allowing for smooth transitions between tense exploration and adrenaline fueled combat sequences  every pressed button matters when you re battling bioengineered monstrosities  and the new inventory system streamlines the experience without sacrificing the strategic aspect that fans have come to love   graphics and atmosphere graphically  the remake is nothing short of breathtaking  the richly detailed environments—ranging from the claustrophobic corridors of the raccoon city police department to the eerie  rain soaked alleyways—are rendered with a level of detail that amplifies the horror  atmospheric lighting  dynamic weather effects  and meticulously designed textures combine to create an environment that feels both real and otherworldly  the constant interplay of light and shadow informs your every decision  making each moment a masterclass in tension building visual storytelling   audio and narrative immersion sound design plays a pivotal role in enhancing the terror of the game  every groan  distant scream  and unsettling silence is masterfully crafted to keep you on the edge of your seat  the adaptive soundtrack changes with the action  blending subtle  haunting melodies with explosive  adrenaline pumping themes during intense moments  beyond the immersive audio and visuals lies a revamped narrative that deepens the lore and emotional stakes of the characters  the dual campaign—following both leon s  kennedy and claire redfield—provides fresh perspectives and intertwines their stories with unexpected twists that pay homage to the original while offering entirely new experiences   innovations and respect for the original what makes this remake exceptional is its ability to honor the original resident evil 2 while introducing groundbreaking innovations  modernized puzzles  upgraded enemy ai  and reworked environmental interactions ensure that every playthrough feels dynamic and challenging  subtle nods to the classic version are interwoven with new narrative layers  creating a rich tapestry that invites both longtime fans and newcomers to explore a dark  intricate world  the score  visuals  and gameplay mechanics all synergize to form an experience that is both a tribute to a landmark in horror and a bold evolution of the genre   conclusion in every sense  resident evil 2 remake is a triumph—a refreshing yet respectful reinvention that amplifies the original s strengths while boldly pushing the envelope of what survival horror can be  it s not merely a nostalgic revisiting of the past  it s a revitalized journey into terror  where every corner conceals a new nightmare and every decision could mean the difference between life and an untimely demise   imagine diving even deeper into the evolution of survival horror—exploring the intricate design choices behind other iconic remakes  or analyzing how narrative depth can reinvent a genre  what aspect of this chilling reinvention resonated the most with you  and which other elements of horror deserve a modern twist

 h1 resident evil 2  a remake fans have been asking for  h1    b resident evil 2  b  is a remake of the 1998 classic that was released on the playstation  fans had long been clamoring for a remake because while the original game was still enjoyable  both its graphics and gameplay had become outdated  the remake aimed to offer a fresh experience to both new and old fans of the resident evil franchise  utilizing the same engine as resident evil 7   b resident evil 2  b  attempted to recapture the essence of the original while providing modern visuals and maintaining its roots in survival horror   in this game  players take on the roles of either leon s  kennedy on his first day as a police officer or claire redfield in search of her brother  both set within the raccoon police station   b resident evil 2  b  features two scenarios  denoted as a and b scenarios  which can be played by two different characters  resulting in a total of four campaigns that can be completed  while the story doesn t differ significantly based on the character you choose  the narrative is closely tied to the scenarios  it s enjoyable to see the different perspectives and interactions of the characters with their environment  and the canon story can be interpreted by the player  as the final outcome doesn t hold much bearing on the overarching story   with  b resident evil 2  b   after venturing into more action oriented gameplay in resident evil 4 and returning to old school horror in resident evil 7  capcom successfully recaptured the essence of what made the resident evil franchise memorable  the remake intensifies the experience compared to the original  especially with the introduction of mr  x  t 00   a towering and relentless pursuer who chases you for a significant portion of the game  whereas in the original  he only appears in b scenarios  the zombies were also redesigned to be more in line with the original resident evil 2  since resident evil 4  shooting zombies in the head has been the most effective method to eliminate them  and that still holds true in modern resident evil games  however  the remake adopts the classic approach from the original resident evil games where zombies are not easily dispatched  and headshots may lead to critical hits and instant kills  but occurs very rarely  it s often preferred to conserve ammo and avoiding unnecessary combat  particularly because resident evil 2 doesn t provide generous supplies like more recent entries  the horror element is maintained by the constant threat of mr  x  zombies that are difficult to kill  and a scarcity of resources that require careful management    b resident evil 2  b  also incorporates puzzles and hidden items that players can discover  solving these puzzles and finding hidden items provides valuable rewards  such as weapon upgrades or entirely new weapons  it s worth the effort to solve these puzzles since it allows you to experiment with different weapons  notably  leon and claire have different sets of weapons  which prevents the game from becoming repetitive  resources found in the environment often come in raw materials  allowing you to craft your preferred type of ammo or healing items  the return of the item box  a staple in the resident evil series  plays a crucial role in resource management  as you must save and conserve your limited supplies   a noteworthy addition to combat in  b resident evil 2  b  is the use of the knife  while the knife has been an important weapon in various resident evil games  it plays a critical role in  b resident evil 2  b   particularly when dealing with zombie bites  the knife can be used to avoid damage and push zombies away  but it has limited durability and is quite rare to find  once used  it can only be recovered by defeating the zombie that has it  which may require using additional ammo  this creates a balance between protecting yourself from zombie bites and conserving precious ammunition   to the best of my recollection   b resident evil 2  b  does not have any significant downsides  while mr  x s presence can become more of an annoyance than a true threat as you become familiar with the game  he can also be avoided  the game may start to feel repetitive if you play it multiple times to complete both scenarios with different characters without taking breaks  at times   b resident evil 2  b  might feel too similar to the original  to the point where it could be seen as a cash grab with minimal changes  but it s a well deserved remake   in summary   b resident evil 2  b  is a solid remake of the original resident evil 2  1998  that remains faithful to its roots  players who are more accustomed to modern resident evil titles might struggle to adapt to the gameplay mechanics of  b resident evil 2  b   but it serves as a great entry point for understanding what made the resident evil series great in its early days    h2 for some reason  dlc costumes of elza walker for claire are very popular among modders  i wonder why   h2

requires the player to keep paying attention at their ammo and such at all times   simple puzzle solving embedded in some of the most clever level design i ve seen in a game of this type   a completely seamless experience in which you can go from the very starting point all the way to the end without a single loading time   a lot of replayability thanks to the various playable scenarios  difficulty settings   good scares here and there but nothing that would cause a panic attack either meaning the player can keep focusing on playing the game without fearing for cheap jumpscares and such   incredibly beautiful graphics that still hold up a few years later  with ray tracing and fsr support     lots of graphical options as well as great optimisation make this game playable even on weaker hardware while keeping acceptable performances and graphics quality   the in game logic is not tied to the framerate meaning you can go way beyond 60fps without fear of breaking the game landmarks intact  and some of the most legendary bosses in the franchise  re2 is easily a 9 10 in my book  and a must play for anyone old enough to handle gruesome stuff

zombie can take upwards of 15 bullets to fully die  which is far from sustainable in a game where ammo is as scarce as it is  you have to choose whether to shoot them in the head once to stun them and squeeze by  or spend the ammo on killing them if you think they re gonna be a recurring threat  ㅤthe puzzles are fine  they re nowhere near as  i puzzling  i  as the ones in resident evil 1 hd other   trial   error until the thing happens  or a 3 digit combination that can easily be written down and regurgitated on repeat playthroughs  ㅤspeaking of repeat playthroughs  there are  2nd run  variants of each character s story  in the original resident evil 2  you d play your first run as one character  e g  claire a  and then you d play your second run as the other character  e g  leon b  and events from your first run would carry over to the second  it s genuinely a cool idea for replayability  but it s where the remake really drops the ball  2nd runs in the remake are mostly identical to 1st runs  you start in a different place but still run through the game mostly the same as the 1st run  just with item and enemy positions scrambled  some 3 digit combinations are also changed  but you can just write those down too  you also get a  secret  final boss and ending for beating the 2nd run  which is by far the most substantial difference  overall  i d say 2nd runs are basically  it punishes you for doing well and rewards you for playing like ♥♥♥♥  it makes sense  if you re huffing sharpie fumes  maybe  it s just so ♥♥♥♥♥♥♥ frustrating when i headshot a zombie and i must say i really loved this game  the overriding feeling was that i always i had just the right amount of resources left to keep going  it s an incredibly difficult balance to strike  as most of the time games and up giving you far too much or too little  it s painstakingly crafted  with enemies  puzzles and resources arranged perfectly  it always felt fair  never harsh for the sake of being harsh    it had an incredible sense of fun  concise resource management and satisfying problem solving  crafting was kept simple and to a minimum  just like it should be  and the same goes for weapon customization  the art direction and mechanics were god tier  and teams of voice actors body face models were excellent    my biggest gripe was mr x  i appreciated how he spiced things up and forced you to draw on your knowledge of the map and improvise  but i would have preferred if he was only there for a small section  instead of totally inverting the game  a little goes a long way after all  it s not a big deal on pc  as there is a mod to remove him  which i did after leaving the police station  initially i also wasn t a huge fan of the sewers  nor the lab  but after subsequent playthroughs they did feel a bit better    a lot of people complained about the adaptive difficulty  but i liked it as it prevented the game from becoming too easy once i got better  there is also a mod to remove this feature if you don t like it    9 10

  on all of them   first off  it s extremely well balanced   the game is generous enough to give you exactly what you need  but not so much that it s boring because it will immediately challenge you resident evil 2  2019  is a perfect example of a remake that successfully adapts to modern game standards while keeping the original resident evil experience alive   the game is set around a zombie outbreak in raccoon city  released by the umbrella company  which developed several zombie viruses to be used as biological weapon  you can play as one of two main characters  leon or claire  who get trapped in the police station of raccoon city  they then need to solve puzzles  fight zombies and unravel the mystery around umbrella and the zombie virus   i can t say much about balance as i only know how the game worked for me personally  but i was amazed how well it worked  as a survival horror game  you constantly struggle with ressources   mainly ammo  there were a few parts in which i completely ran out of ammo and had to progress further while completely changing my playstyle  while this was an intense experience it didn t make the game impossible and i could always recover myself   another part i really enjoyed was exploring the map and solving the nested puzzles  which often had their own really enjoyable mini games as the portable safes or some wave syncing hacking device   lastly combat is the third big part in the game and also incredibly satisfying as the guns feel very responsive and the zombies are animated physically  you can even shoot at their legs to make them faceplant and continue crawling at you   there is a part in the game in which you are searched and chased for by an undying monster while you need to continue exploring and solving puzzles in the police station  as you know most of the station very well at that time  its maybe a nice idea to push the player to hurry a bit  but for me it felt very annoying after just a few minutes  when the horror effect was gone   speaking of horror  the game delivers a very decent horror atmosphere with occasional gory scenes and some good  yet sometimes very predictable jump scares  however the game managed to scare me even when i knew that this zombie hanging at the ceiling will drop at some point in the game  because it distracted me with another heavy enemy just before that   after playing the game the first time as leon  i expected claired story to be significantly different  it turned out  that the story itself is different  but with the same levels and puzzles most of the time  as i met claire outside of the police station in leons story  i wondered what she did in the meantime  but in her story she was inside the station and leon outside   so  yeah  i was a bit disappointed about that   all in all  everything in the game was fun  walking around  shooting at zombies  playing with puzzles  collecting and organizing ressources  the sound and graphics build a great atmosphere and the story is worth uncovering   definitely play this game if you are not too scared of dark rooms and zombies

been said about what this game does right  so i ll focus on the things that stood out to me   the zombie shooting is industry leading in my opinion   other games let you do more damage to a zombie s body or are more action packed  but re2 really hits a sweet spot between gruesome damage  enemy reactivity  and atmosphere that results in every fight having this feeling of tension and horror as heads snap back and flesh is blasted from bone but they just keep coming   the combat has a methodical pace that really works in the game s favour  with every bullet being an uncertain experiment  hoping this is the one that puts your enemy down as you start to backpedal   i usually get numbed to the violence of zombie slaying pretty quickly in games that feature them  but re2 persistently unsettled me   claire s story was unexpectedly impactful   i ve played a looot of resident evil games and this isn t a franchise i come to for stories that make me feel something  but there was a simple yet well executed setup and payoff here that has left a lingering emotional impression   pleasantly surprised here   the map is fantastic   more games that involve backtracking  i need  i  to take some lessons from the exhaustive  thorough  and helpful information presented by re2 s highly readable map screen   that said  i do have some lingering gripes   the problems that pushed me away from the game in the first place are still there  i m just glad i pushed through them this time   i understand people love the mr  x pursuit mechanic but it just struck me as frustrating   you don t to play smart  note his position in the police station  and do stuff away from him  only to have him appear on top of me out of nowhere while i was in the middle of something  forcing me to go on another time consuming safehouse run   it just wore out its welcome quickly   i don t think the lickers in this game work  and you run into a lot of them   the game encourages you to exploit their blindness and sneak past them  but the hallways are tight  and when i ve seen one on a ceiling  i ve only managed to sneak past by walking maybe twice   they always seem to detect me despite my best efforts  resulting in a fight that invariably chews through a lot of ammunition that i can t get back   i had a much better time with the blind enemies in re4   the conclusion here is that i m really happy i pushed through my gripes with this one and finished it  because holy hell is this a good game   heartily recommended to anyone that loves survival horror  and if you re having trouble with mr  x  my advice is to focus on advancing the story until he s no longer an issue  because it s worth persisting

great game with excellent graphics and incredibly optimized pc port  re2 remake is one of the games that redefined modern capcom games alongside dmc5   they did an excellent job with the character designs  the representation of the map and exploration based on the original game and adapting the controls to the new modern look and camera control  it s a good game  offers good challenge and requires exploring a lot  it takes the survivor horror genre as is should  being limited in resources like health and ammo and requiring deep exploration to unlock new areas to progress  the game doesn t tell anything and doesn t indicate where to go or what you should do  it s all about surviving on your own and grabbing new information from items   the graphics and cinematography of the game are really good and show the power of the re engine  but the most impressive of all is how optimized the game is  it ran incredibly well here with super smooth frame rates at 1440p 60fps  everything maxed out on a rtx 2060 super  everything looks amazing and the graphics menu is very complete with several options to choose  i didn t encounter any frame rate drops  stutters or bugs  very polished and flawless experience  the engine also scales incredibly well from really high end hardware running everything maxed out to low end hardware running at lower settings but still being acceptable   my only complaint is that the game is very short  took me about 4h to beat the first time with leon and 2 5h to beat the second time with claire  if you count the pause time  messing with menus  cutscenes and replaying some sections after dying  both playthroughs took a total of 10 hours for me  it s interesting to be able to play as two characters  everything is mostly the same with a few changes but i find it necessary to play with both claire and leon in order to fully understand the story   playing route a with a character then route b with the other is recommended  there are also a few free dlcs that should add an hour or two more   if you like speedrunning  re2 remake is basically built around this and gives incentives to the player to finish the campaign as fast as possible to get higher rank at the end   the game is very polished and a high quality experience  just beware that it s very short  i recommend buying when it s on sale

 h1  wow   where to begin    h1   resident evil 2 shows us how remakes should look like  there is a good reason why rewiews are overwhelmingly positive   it s because the game makes both fans of the old school  original re games  as well as newcomers to the series  like myself  happy  modern third person shooter mechanics get combined with classic puzzles without being a convoluted mess  rather  they complement each other beautifully  but let s get into some details    h1  gameplay   h1  the game can be separated into three big sections  the first third of the game is spent inside the raccoon police station  and this is where you find the classic re puzzles  locked doors with spades  hearts and diamonds engraved into them  and corresponding keys wanting to be found  a gas leak  which needs you to find a valve  a statue which you need to hand a book so it releases the cepter it s holding  the list goes on  while this may sound tedious on paper  the execution is done very well  after all  you re not just running around in an empty building   you need to watch your step  as behind every corner danger might be lurking   what really enhances gameplay is our next big point     h1  difficulty   h1   the way this game handles difficulty is phenomenal  it is far  far more than just a health slider for enemies  though enemies are tougher or weaker depening on the difficulty   when playing the game for the first time  you will likely find yourself short on ammunition   pretty much all the time  enemies seem to be everywhere  and it might look like zombies refuse to stay down for good most of the time  the locked doors imentioned  paired with safes and locks you don t have the combination for right away don t exactly help your cause  so why is this a good thing  again  because the game is only as difficult as your ability to adapt  this isn t the kind of  we re simply throwing more and more enemies your way  difficulty  but rather the approach dark souls takes  yes  i compared the game to dark souls  i m sorry and i promise i m not a game journalist   difficulty comes from your lack of knowledge  when playing the game for a second time  you ll know where the enemies are  you ll know what the safe combinations are  you know what to expect from each boss fight  and that is where a lot of the fun comes from  learning how the game works  learning which enemies to take out and which to simply run past  where you find key items and so on  a very rewarding  almost rouge like experience  in addition  standard mode has adaptive difficulty  if the game notices you re struggling  it goes a bit more easy on you  enemies have lower health  move more slowly and sometimes they don t even show up in the first place  on the flipside  if you re doing well  are always in good health and loaded with ammo  the training wheels come off  bit by bit    h1  story   h1  re2 tells the story of two survivors  claire and leon  no matter which one you decide to play first  personally i recommend leon   you re going to   as the game puts it   experience the nightmare first hand  be it leon  who slowly uncovers who or what is behind the zombie outbreak or claire  who  after finding out her brother wasn t in town originally had no reason to stick around  takes a detour to help a very special person she met along the way  while the story stays faithful to the original for the most part  some details and characters were changed  most of which help show how grim things really are in raccoon city   spoiler  looking at you  kendo       spoiler    h1  conclusio   h1   i cannot recommend this game enough  you ll be scared  you ll be disgusted  you might even get frustrated   but at the end of the day  you will probably agree with me that this game is well worth the time and money     h1  post sciptum   h1  i said you ll be disgusted and scared  and rightfully so  the game really doesn t hold back to both show and tell you horrible depravities  but all the zombies  mutated monsters and infuritating emails left by corrupt policemen and rithless scientists pale in comparison to one  single note you can find while playing as sherry or claire  it s a diary entry on a table next to a typewriter in iron s taxydermy room  in the orphanage  this one note   really messed me up  it s not very long but it cuts a lot deeper than anything else i ve seen in this game  heck maybe even in any game  i will not spoil it here  if you know  you know  if you don t you can search for it yourself  but once you find it   maybe it s best you don t read it

 h1 resident evil 2  h1  a re imagining of the third person  survival horror behemoth  that is resident evil 2  this rendition is an authentic old school experience in the modern era of gaming  it brings the classic quirks and charms of the original into capcom s new re engine that significantly improves the visual and audio fidelity alongside mechanical refinements and more  resulting in frightening moment to moment confrontations  great atmospheric tension  and a plethora of scares     h1 visuals  h1  the visuals are astonishingly photorealistic  the art department nailed everything from the design and creation of assets to the texturing and lighting of the environment  everything feels meticulously designed with tons of passion for not only resident evil but also horror as a genre  resulting in exploration and confrontations being unsettling  captivating  and fulfilling   the zombies are terrifying and distinctive enough to avoid feeling generic or repetitive  whether it is a recently transformed victim  an infected creature  or the tyrant himself  a characteristic they all share is the frightening and impressive designs  from npcs to the police force or your two main characters  the models look great and are recognizable     h1 soundtrack  h1  audibly the game sounds incredible in just about every way  for example  the weaponry  from reloading your magazine to blasting a zombie s head off  does not get stale because of how realistic and satisfying it sounds  that is if you can distract yourself from the spine chilling zombie groans and ambient noises  which is enhanced by the soundtrack as it keeps you on your toes by eeriness or an intense moment  amidst all  the voice actors portray their feelings and intentions through interactions and voice lines encompassing despair and distress to heartfelt moments    h1 gameplay  h1  through gameplay  most of the  old school  qualities come into play  as it plays like a third person horror game from that era when the original was released  but with a modern twist   rather than revamping the formula  capcom decided to stick true to the original  which is fine  and possibly preferred by many  that does  however  mean that the kinks and quirks also remain   exploration and inventory management are among the most vital aspects of the gameplay loop  resources are scarce and thus require you to think twice about what you take with you and when to use it  or whether to blast a zombie to bits or try to run away  it incentivizes exploration as this is where you can find resources such as ammunition  medkits  clues  notes  and upgrades for your guns or inventory  exploration is further encouraged through the thrilling and stunning visuals that racoon city has and ultimately makes it enjoyable    backtracking is also a massive part of resident evil 2  whether because of an item you need to progress the story  complete a previously inaccessible puzzle  a lack of inventory storage  or mr  x forcefully causing you to retreat to the nearest safe zone  luckily  the map layout is easily readable and manageable  but that does not stop it from occasionally becoming a drag or a nuisance   i also found mr  x to be incredibly obnoxious  which he is intended to be  but to the extent that i felt it was hurting my enjoyment of the game as well as not being fond of the early stage inventory storage limit finding it obtrusive rather than a mechanic to work around  it  however  slowly becomes a null issue later on    combat is incredibly satisfying and entertaining  whether you are conservative with your ammunition and try to avoid using it or being trigger happy mowing down every zombie you path into  zombies are a bit hard to take down and require a respectable amount of bullets to finish off  although it is possible to slow and immobilize them by either shooting at their limbs or utilizing a flashbang  they do manage to avoid feeling bullet spongey   that is  however  the polar opposite of boss fights  they generally feel extremely clunky  unfun  bullet spongey  and frustrating  they are memorable  but not because of the battle itself  but because of the visual design of the bosses and the soundtrack that comes with it  that is where the art and audio department carries the moment and makes it memorable     h1 story  h1  a virus engulfs raccoon city  causing chaos amongst its citizens  as its victims arise as flesh eating zombies that roam the streets  the story revolves around leon kennedy and claire redfield  they have different motives and intentions but ultimately are intertwined in the overarching story  claire is on the hunt for her missing brother  chris redfield  whereas leon kennedy is a new police officer who has his precinct completely wiped and tries to get to the bottom of what happened and who caused it   it is at heart  a resident evil story and by no means perfect  nor does it pretend to be  there is  however  plenty of character and fun to behold within it  it may be as cheesy and cliche as it comes  but it is incredibly entertaining to witness claire and leon s stories play out    h1 additional note s    h1  resident evil 2 can be considered a short game  but has replayability alongside bonus content such as additional events  bonus missions  unlockables  and more    h1  final thoughts   h1  a faithful re imagining worthy of being played by newcomers and veteran fans of the series  it is visually and audibly stunning with a cheesy  albeit memorable story and characters  action is satisfying and fun while still being a tension filled survival horror game  the minor issues i have are the clunky boss fights and how incredibly intrusive mr  x can feel  but despite that  i highly recommend playing it risen into the ranks amongst my favourite games of all time   i don t think i ve ever had an experience that so effectively managed to make me simultaneously want to explore every inch of its occasions that made my heart race like absolute crazy  even from menial things such as sound cues  lighting or ambience  i truly could go on about this game for hours  i considered starting a youtube and ambience   u  this game mixes survival horror and action in the most perfect of ways  without ever feeling like the action movie oriented entries later down in the franchise  every corner of rpd has atmosphere in spades  all accompanied by haunting ambience  faint background noises off in the distance  and a substantial lack of cliched horror movie esque sounds music    u  exploration and collecting   u  the level in which this game is crafted to prod the player s ingenuity and make them think about things such as item management  crafting  storage and route planning on a consistent resident evil 2 is an official remake of the game with the same name released in 1998  the remake version utilizes over the shoulder view like most third person games nowadays  rather than the fixed camera view in the original  in this game you play as leon and claire  2 survivors of the zombie outbreak  while both characters have their own story modes  honestly it feels more like a  what if  scenario  since both campaigns use the same maps and similar puzzles  with only some unique areas and meeting different npcs being the difference  however  to unlock the true  extended ending you need to play as both characters  one in new game  scenario a  and the other in 2nd run  scenario b    while this game might seem like a linear  third person horror shooter  for me it s more like a puzzle game with survival horror elements  while some parts of the game are linear  usually the  transition  areas   the main story just consists of 3 large  open areas  where you must solve a puzzle to progress  the puzzles in a and b have minor differences  but mostly the same  it s not like  evil within for example  which is a linear shooter  the story for one character s campaign is quite short  and doable in 2 3 hours if you re already familiar  it is also not recommended to try killing all zombies  as they are quite hard to kill even with a headshot  and in the end you re just wasting ammo  if they re knocked down  you can just run past them    there is also a free dlc included  named the ghost survivors  it consists of 5 short  linear missions where you play as dead npcs encountered during the main story in an alternate scenario  there are no puzzles here  just lots of enemies to fight and avoid  3 of them can be played in an easier difficulty  while the other 2 only has one difficulty option and are both really difficult  if you like survival horror shooters  you ll like resident evil 2  just keep in mind that you need to manage your inventory and ammo 
excellent remake that is both faithful to its source material and creative with its new adaptation  great replay value with multiple paths and character runs 
this is a perfect example of what games should be  it inspires a wide range of emotions and attitudes through multiple playthroughs  it isn t a simulation and you will be frustrated with it if you try to play it like a true cinematic experience or roleplaying game  this is a game with strange rules you adapt to in order to win  if you re bad at this  the game is made for you  it will be scary but once you get better it feels like a gross action movie  only recommend this to players that are okay with having a bad time  not for everyone and not really something that makes you feel good when you play it 
this is the way to do a remake  as a big fan of the resident evil franchise i really enjoyed this gem  my recommendations to everyone  i just hope they will do re4 remake as good as this one    ovako se radi rimejk  kao veliki fan resident evil a jako sam uzivao u ovoj igri  moje preporuke svima  samo se nadam da ce re4 rimejk biti dobar kao i ovaj

easily one of the best resident evil titles to date    expert level design with interesting locations and fun puzzles   badass monster designs and terrifying zombies  marking the first time zombies have actually been scary in a long time   tons of replayability that can easily keep you entertained long after your first run through the story   incredible graphics that take full advantage of the re engine to deliver jaw dropping and shocking visuals   i am biased because of my love for resident evil in general  but this game really is something to behold  i think any newcomer could start with this game as a gateway into the resident evil franchise   easily in the running for best resident evil  as well as best survival horror game of all time  it s a blast through and through and i recommend to any and everyone
