remakes are complicated   i m not instinctively interested in them  primarily because i ll usually gravitate towards the original game  there s just an element of the original article that s more compelling  right  in spite of the antiquated looks or cumbersome control schemes  i d prefer to engage directly with the game as it was first conceived   games don t diminish with time  even as technological progress widens the gap  we don t need to always be yanking the past into the present with its classics  beyond strictly commercial reasons  i guess    one of the defining features of the remake  graphics quality  has never acted as an impediment to immersion for me  indeed  there s been occasions where the visual makeover acts as a counteragent to that immersion  in such cases i find myself  i less  i  absorbed in a game s setting  not more   but i understand that for newer players  the original appearance is the impediment  so i m glad the remake is there to serve them   it has other purposes  though  and i think one such remake exemplifies them in every respect  that game is  i resident evil  remake  i   2002    it s the perfect  i resi  i  game in so many ways  radically improving the original whilst remaining true to its essential essence  if you put  i resident evil  i  and  i resident evil  remake  i  in front of me  i d opt to play the latter—a rare circumstance for me  it s distinguished by the fact that their creator  shinji mikami  oversaw both titles   2019 s  i resident evil 2  i  is not  i resident evil  remake  i   which is to say it does not surpass its 1998 progenitor    but it comes very close  and that is wild  because the 1998 version is one of the best games ever made   in a major shift   i resident evil 2  i  switches out the fixed camera for an over the shoulder third person perspective  that doesn t mean that the journey through raccoon city s police department is closer to  i resident evil 4  i  than  i 2  i   though   this remake is a genuinely frightening game  in which the radically updated visuals bring the zombie infested police station new  un life  the stunning use of light and grunge work to its advantage  it doesn t have the same ambient strengths as the original  which didn t much need the dark to elicit fear  but that s okay—what it lacks in originality it more than makes up for in efficacy   this is a gorgeous game that works as a startling piece of video game horror   its most successful feature is mr  x  he was strictly a scripted terror in the original game  but here he roams the police station in search of you  what an incredibly brilliant piece of design his presence is  too  he changes the way in which you approach navigation  requiring you to listen closely to the environment so as to avoid a costly encounter should he prove too close   it s astounding to me that the follow up   i resident evil 3  i   2020   dropped the ball so badly in expanding this concept to nemesis himself  instead of being an evolution on the roaming terror of mr  x  nemesis was reduced to a series of uninspired boss fights  but then the  i resident evil  i  series has often taken one step forward  two steps back  see  i resident evil 4  i  and  i 5  i     i think this game drops in quality a bit once you leave the police station  which i d argue wasn t necessarily the case before  but that might just be because high tech laboratories are less interesting  and more predictable   settings   with this  i resident evil 2  i  we also see the shift from earnest  so bad it s good writing to the slick but mediocre writing that started in  i resident evil 7  i   it s very much like watching a competent but substandard horror  where the characters are dull  the writing boring  and the performances predictable   i m not going to argue  i resident evil  i  should be more like it “used to be”  because the writing was terrible  but you can t deny it had an identity of its own  does  i resident evil 2  i   2019  have such an identity  despite largely staying true to the original  i don t think it does  at least not as a piece of narrative  and that s kind of weird  like i said  though…     remakes are complicated   this one works best if you set aside the original  they are two different experiences  here you have a high quality journey all of its own that compliments its past  rather than seek to usurp it  it can t really  i be  i  the original and nor should it  instead setting out to adapt the concept for modern audiences   and why not  right  again  it s without its own singular brilliance in that modernity has rendered its narrative content bland  but i m willing to forgive that when it plays this damn well   i won t pick  i resident evil 2  i   2019  over 1998 s masterpiece  but i remind myself that i don t have to  i ll visit this rendition of raccoon city for its visceral horror  its sinister mr  x  and its refinement of style  both in play as well as appearance   if only all remakes were this good  i might find myself enjoying more of them 
edit november 2022  changed to positive review due to capcom creating a beta dx11 version of the game  which works with windows 7 64 bit  i managed to significantly improve the 5 15 second freezing bug  i was believe it or not  trying to solve this bug for the last 2 years  anyways  it turns out the dram  cpu  nvidia driver and game are fighting with each other regarding vsync and textures  so here s the solution to stop them fighting  1  in nvidia control panel set vsync off or even better adaptive  half refresh rate    not application controlled   and set texture to quality or high quality 2  in game set refresh rate to 60 3  in game set frame rate to  variable 4  in game set vsync to on  crazy but it worked for me  5  graphics settings choose a pre set  recommended or max  and do not modify  except as a last resort  6  a sign that you have been sorted is the logos fps jumps from single digit fps to double digit  uptp 80fps    hope this helps if you have win7 and nvidia card 7  if the above haven t worked go to the bios  a  increase the dram voltage especially if you have an old system  helps ram to sustain speed otherwise system freezes during intensive level loading   e g increased mine from 1 95 to 1 975  check manufacturer max volt and don t exceed  b  increase the cpu voltage slightly e g mine increased from 1 2250 to 1 2500  in case your cpu is under volted it will increase performance    this helped me a lot c  disable cpu virtualisation  disable all cpu powersaving media functions 8  disable any gpu overclocking resident program  eg gpu tweak ii etc  9  fire up one of your other favourite games which have no freezing issues  in my case t7   you will be surprised that your favourite game that you thought never freezes does actually freeze periodically depending on the above settings  check this game  t7  from time to time  it helped me diagnose the freezing issue for re2  i later found that my gpu clocking program had to be turned off as well as coretemp  10  change your desktop resolution to match that of the game 11  install reshad exe mod 12  try steam offline mode 13  disconnect from internet and disable antivirus  14  some people reported that disconnecting unused usb devices helped    edit october 2022  latest update is windows 10 only  so much for a remake of an old classic with film grain etc had to install dx11 beta but now the notorious 5 15 bug is back  the whole system literally freezes for 5 seconds every fifteen  best case    absolutely nothing will remove this bug this time even when graphics is set to recommended or max  v sync enabled or disabled  no avail what a shame i thought i overcame the 5 15 bug only for the update to bring it back  old review  pros graphics  voice acting top notch 10 10 story 9 10 sound effects  music 8 5 10 action 7 10 shooting satisfaction 5 10  trying to headshot wobbling enemies  as weapons are upgraded so too are the enemies     cons all the cons stem from the dreaded very low frame rate affliction  let me explain  1  the game is engrained with single room hdd loading system as this was the method for the original resident evil series  therefore the game almost freezes when moving between rooms or areas as it intensively accesses your drive  unless you have installed the game on a top notch m 2 or nvme storage device   it prefers to frequently load in small batches   it simply doesn t know to load the adjacent rooms areas on your ram like sotr which takes ages loading multiple areas but overall better loading experience  2  when turning down the graphics  the game wrongly assumes you want to transfer graphics processing to the cpu which is a killer for older systems with new graphics  old phenom  8gb ram  gtx 1070 8g   turning down all graphics gave me 1 2 fps  yes all graphics turned down made the game slower   this nearly drove me nuts until i defaulted to recommended graphics  high  by mistake and lo and behold i could run the game at 30 40 fps again  3  if you are fighting enemies between areas  game slows down as it tries to load data from drive making beating enemies more difficult
police station gave me a headache  cool game tho 
tldr  i m a fanboy and you should buy it   i ve been playing resident evil for 20 years  and this is my favorite  i played it first on ps4 pro  pre ordered  but now that i have a pc and it was on sale  i decided to buy it again for the mods  my first playthrough was as third person claire  now i am running both leon campaigns with the first person mod by praydog  having a flashlight toggle and an upclose view of everything has changed the game for me  so even if you own it on console  it s worth buying again for the mods   the game itself is utter brilliance and seems to address all the main complaints of re4 7  and to some degree the first three games  the fixed camera angles of the old games were cool  but combat suffered in my opinion due to autoaim  meaning in replays  the scare factor is gone and it s a matter of whether you have enough distance and ammo  with re4  5  and 6  aiming is nice    but too nice  simply put  those are action games with mostly smooth controls  predictable enemy movements and exploitable ai  and re7 was pretty good  brought back classic puzzle design  revamped the horror and had an aiming system that felt good for re  tanky  slow  with enemy movements that made it hard to line up a headshot  my biggest complaint was that enemy variety is non existent    this is where re2 remake shines for the same reasons the original did  people were scared this was gonna be another action zombie game  but that couldn t be farther from the truth  these have to be the tankiest  most tenacious zombies ever created  using the adaptive difficulty like re4 did  the game tracks how well or bad you are doing  i ve shot zombies point blank in the face with a shotgun  didn t even stagger the sob just to have it bite me and bring me down from green to red  yikes  but at some point  i ll shoot a zombie once with a handgun and blow their head to bits  and that s how rng works in this game  i never feel comfortable in combat  and that s how it s supposed to be  aiming is hard  zombies sway and stumble towards you  never keeping their head still more than a second at a time  with the way the reticle stays big until you focus  trying to take rapid shots with any weapon will almost always result in wasting ammo  couple this with the fact that unless you get a critical headshot  board all the windows  and clear every room  zombies will almost always get back up  repopulate  and be in an entirely different spot or even different rooms the next time you come through an area  the only thing that stays consistent when playing the game is item locations  everything else beyond cutscenes and certain scripted events are entirely dynamic   resident evil 2 is packed with zombies  lickers  dogs  mr  x  poisonous abominations  not so local flora  and things that just don t seem to die  the puzzles are fairly decent  nothing quite nintendo level  i guess kids can handle harder puzzles than adults but o k  but they re fun to figure out  the game hosts 4 different playthroughs and some extra modes  and is extremely replayable  each playthough only takes 3 8 hours depending on your skill  and that makes replaying it all that much better in my opinion  it s very fun learning from your mistakes to improve your runs  beat the game in less time  with less mistakes  less ammo  it s just that good  buy it   1  re2 remake  2  re hd  3  re 4  4  re 3 classic  re 3 remake just    disappoints     5  re 7  6  re 5  7  residumb ewwvil 6
tldr  highly recommended  this game is for new  and old  fans alike  the game runs exceptionally well with a mid range graphics card  and there s a plethora of graphical settings to tinker around with  the story is a hybrid of  adaptation  and  retelling  of the 1998 original  the voice acting is solid  although it is weird to hear claire and leon swearing during cutscenes and other dialogue  when compared to the original  it s not an overall detraction from the gameplay itself   the addition of directional sound  when wearing headphones  really adds to the experience  and i d recommend playing with headphones if possible  there s also a ton of content when compared to  other  recent remakes in the series  and that was definitely a selling point for me

excelente remake  da justo una mezcla de nostalgia y asombro  una nueva mirada a un clásico 
a primera vista este juego tiene muchos elementos cautivantes  el motor es espectacular  la gráfica y el ambiente son atrapantes y las cinemáticas definitivamente le aportan una connotación más profesional en comparación a la versión anterior de 1998  muy criticada por sus  actuaciones   la primera impresión que te deja este juego puede llegar a ser intimidante en el buen y mal sentido  es reconfortante y desolador reconocer los antiguos pasillos y ser testigo de los maravillosos cambios que el nuevo motor se luce en renderizar  esta delicia definitivamente se acentúa si ya se ha jugado a los originales  para personas nuevas a la franquicia entiendo que no hay mucho nuevo que ver aquí más que el legado de la marca resident evil   y es por esto que debo advertir que el grave problema de este juego  es que cuando se pasa esa etapa inicial de admiración y horror del bueno  gran trabajo de ambiente   viene una segunda etapa de frustración por como han envejecido ciertas mecánicas del survival horror  para veteranos de este género todo ésto no es nada nuevo  pero para los recién llegados  la mayoría del gameplay en este caso se trata de un ejercicio de prueba y error cada vez que se descubre un objeto importante y un lugar nuevo  donde más que asustado puede que provoque aburrimiento de tener que recargar tu fila una y otra vez por un sinfín de situaciones ridículas e injustas que pueden provocar un gameover por un solo error incluso en dificultad estándard   para más remate los enemigos son tremendamente rescilientes hasta decir basta  los recursos son sadísticamente escasos  y los pasos audiblemente auguriosos de cierto personaje que comenzará a frustrar tus planes se vuelven psicológicamente pesados  por eso es que siepre va a ser recomendable no tratar este juego como un festín de balaceras y disfrutarlo por lo que es    el tecer paso  es que una vez que se superan las frustraciones y  haz tenido la paciencia de  aprender de memoria estas injustas papas y todas las maleterías del juego  es posible evitar muchas de las trampas  e incluso disfrutar de todas esa basura  que te plantean cualquiera de las dos campañas  que dicho sea de paso  y aquí se cae de tropezón el juego  son prácticamente lo mismo a excepción de ciertos lugares y eventos    y es que debo quejarme de que los puzzles son los mismos para ambos personajes  con ligeras diferencias en el escenario  b      rutas secundarias  llamadas aquí  que  a propósito  son un despropósito  la cohesión de los eventos está muy mal trabajado en comparación a la versión original de psx  y si jugaste el original esta falta de atención al detalle te chocará bastante   de todas maneras y para concluir  para fans de la serie  es posible que caigan en el cuarto paso del vicio de hacer speedrunning  para casuales  es igual un tremendo juego e indiscutiblemente memorable por diversas razones  sólo estén advertidos  este juega peca de tener linaje de la vieja escuela  ya que avanzar no se trata de entrar como rambo disparando a cada habitación  si no de medir tus acciones y ser silencioso en tus pasos  correr muchas veces es la opción   como nota al pié  acotar que los minijuegos también son tremendamente adictivos pero aún no los completo todos  mis 90  horas de juego se atribuyen a querer completar las campañas con calificación s  para desbloquear todas las armas   incluso fuera de la campaña encontrarás mucho replay value aquí si eres paciente   el tener que comprar la música original  y los trajes adicionales también  en serio capcom   me parece un insulto si y no puedo dejar pasarlo    en fin  un tyrant que se teletransporta por la estación y te encuentra igual en la misma habitación que un licker 10  a veces es muy injusto

in 1998 i bought the original re2 using all my baptism gift money  i literally went to the game store minutes after the ceremony  and used the only 50 bucks i d just received to buy a brand new copy  i raced home to play it and opened the walkthrough guide from psm  my favorite gaming magazine at the time  to be my constant companion  i barely ever got new games as a kid and this was a big deal    even though the graphics were primitive by today s standards  the characters imposed on top of pre rendered backgrounds made for nothing else like it on playstation at the time  looking back now it would be easy to scoff at  but back then it was scary  environments were nerve racking  the odd tank controls and camera angles  the sound design  the character design  the perfectly timed jump scares when everything was quiet    it was all just awesome and kept you on edge constantly   fast forward twenty years and my initial impression is almost the same as with the original  the truck driver is still a fat slob but now i have to watch him eat a greasy cheeseburger before he gets it  leon still has an early beatles haircut for some reason  graphics are stunning but also running flawlessly on a pretty average rig  i swear i had the weirdest deja vu i d ever experienced when i first got into the police department  it was like tons of memories that had been dormant for all those years fired off all at once  it was a truly unique feeling i don t think i d experienced yet in my time playing games  and it made me instantly wish for more remakes of great titles from my youth  i hadn t even gotten into this game yet and already i loved it    your average zombie  while slow and dopey in the 90s fashion  before they turned into olympic sprinters in the early 2000s   are no walk in the park to deal with  they are built like tanks and can easily chew up your already limited ammo  they become even more formidable when grouped together in a narrow spot that makes running by them not an option  to make matters worse  they will often play dead after you put them down  waiting to grab a bite as you walk by later when you backtrack through the area  even multiple headshots above the nose are not always enough to slow them down  in general it is best to leave them alone wherever possible and move around them  because they will clean you out of your inventory otherwise  that said  there are segments where you will wish you had thinned them out more before you had to come through again  find the balance with your play style  you may very well want to reload a save more than once because you used too many rounds in a basic encounter  and you need plenty of ammo despite the fact that you find almost none  you are always down to your last few rounds and wondering how you are going to get through the next fight   there are your other standard re fare enemies and mini bosses to deal with  and they have plenty of hit points too  but all this becomes complicated even more by the fact that you are constantly being pursued by the tyrant  after a little ways into the story  he will reveal himself and follow you through virtually every part of the police station  and into later stages  you have to then complete your objectives  puzzle solving  and looting  all while being inundated with tank zombies in corridors  running dogs and lickers  and while being tirelessly pursued by a giant in a trench coat who can one shot you  it s stressful as hell but still really fun  then  factor in the other things that stuck out in memory from the first time  like being chased down a sewer by a giant crocodile only to come to a conveniently located explosive that you can use against him  it s just a good time and hitting all the nostalgia notes the whole ride    needless to say i recommend this to anyone who enjoyed the original  but someone going their first ever run through will get a really faithful adaptation to the original in capcom aaa form  there are some obvious differences but it s reminiscent of the first in every meaningful way  this game was very close to me when i was a kid and playing the remake has been a trip down memory lane  i hope other game developers take note of its success and start masterfully rehashing some of the heavyweights from past decades

resident evil 2 a year ago i wouldn t believe it  but here i am at around 40 hours playtime with two full playthroughs and the true ending unlocked  not because i ve conquered my fears of horror  at least not yet  rather it was an amazingly terrifying yet thrilling experience from start to finish   the slow transition of creeping through the cold dark hallways of the police station in fear of disturbing the dead to trudging through sewer sludge and mutants on a hellbent heroic mission to storming out of a self destructing underground base mowing down any undead freaks in my way with an epic boss battle conclusion was an exhilarating and oddly natural pace to experience  i enjoyed this type of non linear level design  the chance to go back to old areas to encounter new enemies  new puzzles  new areas  and the most satisfying of them all  new loot    the absolute emotional levels this game can take on you is bizarre  you can feel like an invincible slaying machine if you have the skill and loot for it or be absolutely petrified when low on ammo and health and you hear a certain you know who s boots get louder and louder searching for you  the dopamine release that you get for solving puzzles or finding that cursed part you needed to fix the damn doohickey is on another level of its own  not to mention the boss fights  yes boss fights  this game has  each one more epic than the last   okay  sure the gameplay sounds great but what about the story  which was actually my favorite part  you can play as either rookie cop leon kennedy or college girl claire redfield  some noob nobody and some frat girl you might think  wrong  these two characters are some of the most badass  balls of steel  down to earth people i ve ever seen exist    the basis is  for the primary playthrough  you re rookie cop leon kennedy returning to his hometown  raccoon city  for his first day on the job as a city police officer  that is until he arrives in town with a full on infestation of zombies  mutants  and other horrors  the rookie then takes matters into his own hands and decides to get to the bottom of what s happening in his city with the help of his new friend  claire redfield  together  they are but two of the few survivors left in the now city of the dead  the absolute journey these two go on in just one night blows my mind in how much they were able to accomplish  and what a hell of a night it was   the game has amazing graphics  great voice acting  fantastic gameplay  natural pacing  a good story  deep lore  and lovable characters  the only cons i could find was that the two playthroughs don t make sense together  separate  they re great  but when you start to compare them is when you start running into continuity issues    also  this game is hardcore  there is absolutely no hand holding  the most the game gives you is an assisted mode which helps you out with more loot and weaker enemies but that s it  the puzzles can be extremely hard for new comers and playing hide and go seek with mr  x while not knowing what on earth you re looking for  or if you do where the hell it is  can be rather intimidating  but if you manage to solve it all  kudos  or get help from a guide or friend like i did  makes the whole experience more enjoyable rather than frustrating in my opinion   all in all  amazing game  the price tag is well worth for getting at least two playthroughs  because claire and leon s are both fairly different  or even more for different scenarios  dialogue  achievements  extra modes  dlc  etc  i was lucky enough to pick it up on sale for twenty dollars    nonetheless  this is a game like no other i ve experienced in my decade plus of playing video games  it ll be one to remember for me  which is why i m going to give this game a 9 10  it was horrifically action packed and i loved and hated every moment of it

emotion in the player  and very few are successful at this  amnesia  the dark descent is the best example of a game that makes you afraid  and while the remake does not go to amnesia levels  it played the player entertained and having fun  old school resident evil games operate with this method at their core  and it works out very well  bonus puzzles that are not necessary to continue the game but offer very helpful resources also entice the player to keep looking in every nook and cranny and experience all that the game has to offer   the first resident evil that i played was resident evil 4 killing zombies isn t actually all that fun  and so encouraging the player not to do it all the time lets pressure off of the game for having not so entertaining combat  it is  after all  a horror game  and you should be afraid of the enemies  this remake achieves a good balance of the player s power in that you can obliterate zombies with a shotgun  but you can t kill them all  so you have to immobile in order to reach the best accuracy but staying in one place for too long is an easy way to get overrun  naturally  every shot counts as ammunition is always low and most enemies tear a third videogame remasters are very proliferate  but actual  full remakes are rare  not for the resident evil series  though  capcom has  for better or worse  milked this franchise for years and more than once rather than advancing the storyline they ve engaged into retelling the same story through different perspectives  e g  operation raccoon city  or through a different kind of gameplay  e g  umbrella chronicles   the first actual remake was for the original game  which got a remake for the gamecube  which was later remastered for newer generations   that remake did things right  it took the original concept  setting  story and characters and updated them for the then current technology  the only thing that didn t really change much was the gameplay style  with static cameras  exploration and tank controls  but  after resident evil 4  the gameplay in the series changed  it became more action focused  it relied less in exploration and used an over the shoulder camera that became the standard not just in the series  but in the industry for most action titles   this new remake is a bit of a mix  it takes the re4 style camera  control and gunplay but keeps the exploration focused 3d metroidvania style from the classic games with more focus on survival than action  for the most part  it works wonderfully  but if you re too used to action games you re gonna have a bit of a hard time adapting  enemies are fast  but your character isn t  ammo is scarce and enemies are tough  so you can t solve all your problems by shooting like a gun totting maniac  you need to conserve ammo and get really good at headshots in order to survive  you need to figure out what s the better weapon for every ocassion and you have to swallow your pride and learn to run   one major gimmick of the game is mr  x  while this character  a tall  implacable  seemingly invincible strongman was merely a bit of a mini boss that would ocassionally show up in scripted sequences throughout one of the game s two scenarios  here he shows up in both and he can be terrifying  he will randomly show up when you least expect him and follow you until you manage to lose him or stall him  it can get a bit aggravating  though  when you re trying to just pick up an item or do a puzzle and he shows up and forces you to run away  but as soon as you learn your way through the map and use it to your advantage  you can do well without getting too frustrated  besides  he only shows up in certain sections of the game  so you have more freedom to explore in the rest   the game does a great job of working on its own and as a remake  if you re not familiar with the franchise  this is a good starting point  the game expects you to be familiar with either the first game or the original re2  but it really doeesn t matter if you re not  the story is simple enough  there s a zombie apocalypse in town and you need to find a way to escape   so there s no room for confusion   a possible low point is that the two scenario model of the original game is not as deep here  the original game had you pick one of the scenarios to start and once you finished you d play the second one  while actions taken in the first one would affect the second one  you could make things easier for your first character and harder for the second character or viceversa  depending on your choices   your characters would also ocassionally meet or talk throughout the story and sometimes travel through entirely different areas  if you reversed your original choice and played with the characters in the opposite order you d have new differences in each scenario  in here  though  this is all very minimal  the paths taken in both scenarios are almost identical  your characters only meet once before the end and your actions in the first scenario are irrelevant to the second   there are still enough differences between both characters to keep things fresh  of course  like in the original  playing as each protagonist will have you meet different characters  using different weapons  engaging in different puzzles and ocassionally fighting different bosses   despite the scenario issue  there s a lot of replayability here  certain challenges will help you unlock powerful weapons and new costumes  there are also a bunch of cosmetic unlockables like concept art and models that can be obtained by reaching certain milestones and goals  plus  there are a couple of extra unlockable modes  each with their own characters to play as and their own goals  there are dozens of hours of fun to be had   graphically  it s quite impressive  the lighting  sound and design give the game a creepy atmosphere that no game in the series has managed before  it also runs quite well in middle of the run pcs  it certainly runs better than re7  despite using the same engine and having a few larger areas   it really is quite a phenomenal game  i loved it in the ps4 and bought it on the pc as soon as i could  just in time for the launch of the sequel   you just have to remember that it s not an action game  don t come here expecting resident evil 5 or even 4  but also  don t expect the clunky  static style of the originals  or the remake of the first one

my meaning  i feel backtracking is normally just bad design  but i also believe that sometimes you can break the rules and create something great if the execution is solid   with this game  you re than they help him    this is where the genius of the level design comes into play  everything in this police station all loops back in on itself  so it rewards smarter players to pay attention to how this place is designed  he cannot trap you in a corner if you never get yourself trapped in a corner  the worst that could happen is maybe getting caught by a zombie you might have spared or might not head  is a really bad idea  your main goal should be to slow down the zombies  but i won t technically be giving away strategies or spoilers here  just use your common sense  focus on only killing zombies that you feel really could be a problem with your pathing as you try to avoid x and simply ignore the rest    proper pathing is the key to success in this game  because running into zombies in narrow corridors will almost always cause you to take damage    as you can see  i ve typed all this so far  and it s literally all about me praising the gameplay and design  that should be more than genre    survival horror singleplayer  yes  substantial multiplayer    no campaign    4 6 hrs x 2 campaigns  several mini campaigns replayability   alternate campaigns  extra difficulties  unlocked weapons visuals   aaa  power  excellent design potato mode     surprisingly pretty on lower settings stability     good  framerate dips for bad computers on too high settings genre fans       buy it non genre fans     good enough to maybe change your mind value proposition  strong  even at full price   add ons     mostly cosmetic  none worth your time  the resident evil franchise has blasted itself clean into pop culture knowledge  so there s little need to recite the background here  to summarise  a bad company makes zombie virus bioweapons  and they ve just had a major outbreak  resident evil 2 follows two characters unknowingly driving into a city in the midst of the first major zombie outbreak  taking shelter in a police station that is more than it seems  and stumbling into the corporate conspiracy behind it all  there are effectively two ways to review this game  the first is as a remake of a classic of the genre that defined much of what survival horror would mean  and the second is as someone new to the pre re5 franchise   if you are familiar with the franchise  this is the kind of remake that moves the bar for everyone else working on a remake of the game  it s not a glorified remaster – the entire game has been redesigned from the ground up  with the impressive visual power of the re engine  and the decades of new lessons learned in game design since it first came out  every aspect of the game has been hugely upgraded  the tank controls and fixed camera that were the game s weakest point even at launch are gone  replaced with a third person camera and controls that genuinely feel good to play  the game is no less intimidating for it – being able to aim allows for damage modelling on enemies  especially basic zombies  to introduce entirely new strategic decisions about whether you kill  cripple  or ignore the persistent enemies  weapons all feel good and relevant  and the subweapons work better here than in any other re game    even the narrative has been upgraded  including much better scene flow and removing the most stupid elements of the original  what remains is a tightly interconnected structure with elaborate and complex item puzzles  made far more enjoyable by the use of a map system that flags items left behind  places finished and unfinished  and even ticks off key items you have used up   this isn t a remake of the game to a modern engine  or even a remake of the game as it would have been made were it designed today  this remake is like playing the nostalgic memories of how you vaguely remember it being  except it  i actually exists  i     if this is your first older re game  you ve picked the best way to start  resident evil 2 is a survival horror game that swings between tense combat encounters and item or logic puzzles  effectively  you want to get past the zombies to collect items  which are either basic supplies  upgrades to your equipment  or essential items needed to progress in the game  an essential component of the game is that you don t earn any rewards for killing enemies  and there is mathematically not enough ammunition in the game to kill every single enemy you encounter  but the game world consists of highly interconnected spaces  the enemies are persistent and extremely resilient  and the item puzzles require extensive backtracking  effectively  you have to decide whether you invest the ammo to kill an enemy  invest much less to cripple it and gamble on bypassing it  or simply run past it and gamble on not needing to return to the space  given that the basic zombies in the game can take three to five shots to blow a leg off and anywhere up to twelve headshots to kill for good  that s a genuine decision  spending twenty bullets to clear out a room to find five bullets and an optional clue might not be worth it   in classic survival horror style you explore the game world  using items you find to open up new areas and unlock shortcuts to old ones  finding supplies scattered about to keep going  you might find a weapon locker with a shotgun needing a specific item to unlock  or a safe whose code is written on a paper somewhere in the station  or a door that you need an item to open and continue  at any given time you ll be juggling two to four key items  and with severely limited inventory space you can go light and run short of resources  or go heavy and regularly have to leave items behind  some key items are used once and gone  but many are used repeatedly  luckily  the game will indicate that an item won t be used again and can be discarded  the game also helpfully marks areas you have completely cleared  partially cleared  and not reached yet  as well as key items or doors you have yet to unlock   occasionally the game will mix things up with a new enemy  requiring you to play cautious until you adapt your tactics  or a bossfight  requiring substantial use of ammo and resources to proceed  sometimes an unkillable enemy will pursue you throughout the entire level until you get past a certain point in the story  effectively testing your ability to navigate safely and punishing players who have been too thrifty with their ammo  this enemy can be frustrating to players who don t often play survival horror  so two notes – firstly  it does play fair and actually walks manually around the level  secondly  it detects sound so walking instead of running and avoiding shooting make you much harder to detect   the story may seem a little contrived at times if you are new to the re franchise  but it s no different from most horror movies  where sometimes things just happen because they need to for the story to exist  this has tighter narrative plotting and less hammy voicework or animation than any other re title  so if you can enjoy horror movies you ll be fine here  if you do like horror with a touch of melodrama and ham  this is made for you   there are two campaigns to be played  one for each of the cover characters  each 4 6 hours long depending on your completionism  these are somewhat interconnected and you ll often end up in the same places or fighting similar bosses  but you come from different angles with access to different rooms  and with some entirely unrelated areas  narratively  the campaigns are happening at the same time and place  so you get the complete story only by playing both  the combined campaigns offer pretty good value for money in the genre  the campaigns also offer ‘b sides   which involves shuffling the enemy and boss locations around  and changing a bit of item placement  for generally a more challenging experience  there are also difficulty settings and unlockable weapons to boot  though naturally the overpowered weapons are hidden behind the extremely difficult challenges  some dlc is available in the form of expanded stories  which are of reasonable quality though not to the level of the main events  some connect directly to the narrative and fill out missing spaces  some are just fun asides with a different gameplay experience  and some are a comedic turn on the game  as of writing  this comes free with the main game  and so adds a good few extra hours of play one you ve used the game up  in terms of quality hours to dollars paid  this is the best value for current survival horror games on the market   if you enjoy modern survival horror games  re2 is a great way to experience the principles of classic survival horror with the strength of modern game design  if you played these ‘back in the day   this is a best in class entry from the era  updated with two decades of lessons learned  if you ve never really played survival horror before  if re2 doesn t change your mind and get you playing  it s safe to assume that it s just not your genre

tl dr at the bottom              first  a bit of a story  back in the 90 s  when i was too young to be playing horror games  my parents rented resident evil 2 for the playstation 1 from blockbuster because they loved romero zombie films  however neither of them could figure out how to fire their pistol and kept dying to the first zombie  i then asked if i could give it a try   exasperated  they handed me the controller  it took me about 20 minutes of trial and error before i managed to make it to the gunshop  relatively unscathed    and that was how i began playing my very first m rated video game   my point is the original resident evil 2  released way back in 1998  holds a lot of strong nostalgia for me  i adored that game so much that i collected almost every future release and even read the book adaptations at the library  i fell in love with the series and was very proud to have beaten resident evil 2 100   i had beaten both a and b versions of both characters and unlocked every weapon in the game  even conquering the notorious hunk and tofu modes   so i was pretty nervous with resident evil 2 remake  would it do the original justice  would it feel the same  would it be a completely different beast altogether  would it respect the source   thankfully resident evil 2  does  respect its source material  and actually manages to greatly improve upon it   despite the perspective shifting from old school tank controls to a third person action camera the remake still feels like original resident evil 2  dripping with suspense and terror lined with an oppressive and dark atmosphere  there s a heavy sense of realism despite the over the top plot and crazy action elements  yet it avoids the problems of its predecessors  the action is certainly a spectacle  but doesn t detract from its survival horror core  you still feel like you re fighting for your life  and barely winning  even on the easier difficulties    resident evil 2 still follows the same basic plot beats of the original  but rearranges and even reinterprets a lot of elements  i saw a lot of familiar characters  but found them to be very fresh with brand new performances and even updated motivations and dialogue  rooms  key locations  and enemy behaviours have all slightly changed  not so much that they re unrecognizable but enough that nothing feels copy pasted   for the most part  having played the original up and down  front to back and sideways  i actually recognized a few setups for a surprise scare  i m not saying it s a bad thing to reuse iconic scares from the original  but personally i found they sucked a little of the tension out for me  don t get me wrong  there are plenty of original scares in the remake  but it was very weird being the bravest one of my friends to play this game  especially since i m a massive scaredy cat when it comes to horror games    i guess this isn t really a game review  and more of a love letter to capcom for not screwing up this remake  they honestly didn t need to develop resident evil 2 remake  but i m sure glad they did  they could have made the original an hd port and released it quickly  but this game feels like genuine passion and care was put in by the dev team   an excellent reinterpretation and the closest thing to a perfect survival horror game i ve played in a long long time   please pick up this game  you won t regret it                                 pros  beautifully captures feelings of terror  tension  and suspense of not only the original resident evil 2 but old school survival horror games without falling victim to negative tropes  updates and reinterprets old characters and plot elements in a fresh and respectful manner  everything feels familiar and yet still new  still feels like original resident evil  but won t leave newcomers hanging  graphics are gorgeous  the atmosphere is spot on  tight balance on action and horror  controls are fairly fluid  enemies are challenging and terrifying  damn near perfect survival horror game  cons  not necessarily a bad thing  but having played the original resident evil 2 i recognized some old scares  took away some of the tension  personally i had some issues with the music cutting out  where there was supposed to be music playing there was hardly any sound  may have been patched by now  even on easier difficulties the game can be challenging  which may put off some newcomers  tyrant encounters felt repetitive  made the game a little less scary

an amazing remake of the classic resident evil 2  capcom once again steps things up when it comes to remaking their games  the graphics are the best yet from the re engine  fairly easy to run a smooth 60fps on a variety of hardware  as the engine is very scalable  since the game was designed for consoles which already target 60fps  the pc version benefits from that engine scalability  character models  lighting  etc  are top notch work  with only a few issues such as artifacting  and dithering on some light shadow sources   the developers did a great job of marrying old school design and mechanics with the new school  the camera is no longer static and pre set like the original  but rather behind the shoulder that has become the norm of third person shooters  which was popularized by resident evil 4  they also adapted newer mechanics from the newer re games  like having defensive items to release yourself from an attacker s grasp  the map is hugely improved from past re games and conveys clearer information  keeps track of things you have to investigate  and makes the next objective fairly clear  making it more difficult to get lost  just like the original  the game has extra modes and items you can unlock after finishing the main game  giving it extra replay value for those looking for more   the game is not a one to one remake of the original re2  they redid alot of scenes and took some new liberties with the story and how the events play out  the overall story remains more or less the same compared to the original  with that said  there are some changes that i m not a fan of that i think the original did better  the 2nd run scenario  which is where you can replay the game in a slightly truncated path with items and enemies moved around  feels like an afterthought to the  b  scenario of the original re2  it feels incomplete  as if they had bigger plans but ultimately decided to scrap those ideas  i m also not a fan of some of the choices they made with the story characters and some of the content that was cut from the original  b  scenario of re2  which i won t post here for spoilers sake  while a new player will not notice these changes at all  i think a veteran of re2 will pick up on these changes immediately  overall  i highly recommended it if you played the re and re0 remaster hd and want to continue the story of the series

as we come to closing out the year resident evil 2 has consistently been my goty choice since its release  capcom was able to successfully re imagine a beloved classic with more modern game play mechanics that do not hinder the survival horror experience we fondly remember  while still providing a game that doesn t need nostalgia alone to appeal to a new audience     avoiding zombies when possible and killing only the enemies you need to grab that one key item is usually the best approach  careful use of your weapons is a must especially on higher difficulties as you can find yourself facing down a boss without adequate firepower especially on the hardest difficulty  the game also can be ruthless in that the better you are doing on a run the harder it can get  difficulty scaling based on performance will lead to enemies that take more resources to kill and deal more damage  this is a game at its heart that is designed to destroy any sense of comfort and ultimately try and kill you   the third person combat works really well here  one thing i was worried about was if the introduction of over the shoulder shooting would make the game too easy and eliminate a lot of the challenge of the original game  i can gladly say that is not the case  standing still while aiming nets you better accuracy in that the reticle will shrink providing a more precise shot  the zombies themselves are not guaranteed to be downed by a single shot to the head with basic handguns  that instead is tied to a chance to critically hit which results in a satisfying cranial explosion and feels rewarding when it happens  due to this it is often better to stun one momentarily and run past  of course things like the shotgun and magnum will down your basic zombie with a well placed shot to the face but the ammo is more rare and therefore you want to save it for when its really needed   the game is soaked in atmosphere that instills great dread and the original games gothic police station makes a return here in perfect form  everything from the materials rendered to the lighting makes it one of the most terrifying locations in a horror game  the mystery of this former museum turned police station is intensified by the extremely impressive visuals  capcoms re engine is incredibly well optimized and runs fantastically on even what some would consider  lesser hardware   the puzzles of the original make a return here and are improved  helping you along is a fantastic map system that tracks locations you have and have not entered and any important items you may have missed and may not have had the inventory space for at the time   the story of the game remains largely the same but this time the convincing facial animations  motion capture and vastly improved voice acting help to sell it  it follows the original game very closely while adding a few new interesting segments that differ from the original  it s interesting enough to keep you wanting to progress and as far as resident evil plots go is one of the better ones  sadly though the  zapping system  of the original game is really not present here  before items and weapons could be left behind and used for the other characters b scenario but unfortunately this is missing  the a b scenario itself is also relatively unchanged from your standard run through as a single character  it serves more as a shorter experience of the story rather than a true scenario with its own story beats that change based on the other  overall though it is very good for what it is   on top of all of that there are extra modes that are unlocked and also downloadable content which can be challenging and fun to play  if all you are after is just a single play through it can be a short game  but if you want to do the a b scenarios for both characters as well as the extra content there is quite a bit here to enjoy   no game this year for me has matched the level of suspense that this title has  it s an incredibly immersive experience that should not be missed  it is almost a perfect remake in every way aside from a few small flaws that really aren t deal breaking  just make sure you wear headphones  the sound design is fantastic and it really adds to the experience   stay safe in raccoon city

was his first place of choice  considering the strange deaths occurring just one month ago  while driving to his destination  he finds a corpse on the road  not any corpse though  this one was quite trying to replace some original facts that have previously been settled  such as the events leading leon to raccoon city  where originally he had woken up later from being drunk  now he was apparently just quietly sitting at home waiting to get on duty  probably to appease a more softcore audience  these changes weren t the only ones  some of those changes consist in the alternative routes both protagonists take  but that was to be expected with a remake  their emotional side  however  shines with the modern technology    the game direction choice is on the spot  and the narrative very pleasant  restraining the action to the necessary basis  and not on the ridiculous cheesy stunt level of trying to surpass holywood  cutscenes are worth of a movie with real actors behind each movement  and atmospherically perfect with amazing angles that pushes each scene into waves of vibrant passionate moments   visually   b resident evil 2  b  stays far away as possible from cheap horror components that have been haunting games for a while now  low cost jump scares  complete darkness and the low tier environments  we ve all seen in those claimed horror experiences are nothing compared to what we can experience at raccoon city  instead  players feel the emptiness progressively interrupted by deadly agents  either the mr x or a cleverly placed zombie just slowly walking towards you   this remake may very well have  some of the most beautiful graphics i ve seen  detailed not only at the large scale  but also in small everyday objects  reflexes on the floors are outstanding  the rain effect on the concrete shines naturally  the contrast of the light against the rain over the broken windows is something stunning to look at  not even mentioning the condensation effect present in the greenhouse glass windows  the graphical quality  i capcom  i  achieved is without a doubt a step above the triple a experience we ve got used to  even with other major titles on detailed  with extreme facial expressions of rage  and pure terror  blood is now very real  almost distinguished  from fresh to coagulated  when splattered on walls or floors  gore went back to its fundamental horror themed principle  of a primordial frightening human complex  in a marvelous way  zombies are gruesome  but so it s the ruin of the flesh in its decomposing way  and  i capcom  i faces and sizes remain  other creatures such as lickers were remade to the core  based on the originals  and are looking better than ever   resident evil came along away  from fixed camera angles  to pretty much redefining third person shooters  this remake though  shows us some of the most flawless third person action we came across  with some cons and pros  some of the changes might not please every fan  due to the game s natural adaptive difficulty  this method defines pretty much every action  including the damage inflicted on zombies  although it s still a surviving horror  there are damage critics and damage attributes  that sometimes depend 100  on the game  and not on the player skill  making it a dice roll of chances when trying to accomplish that amazing headshot that splatters a zombie head in two  the difficulty  as well as the critical attributes will define the result  it s clearly a long way since the original  and we can t stomp laying zombies either   this  h1  b review of resident evil 2 pc edition  b   h1        b tl dr  b    resident evil 2 remake is a beautifully done game in its own right  adding gameplay features from across the franchise to create an amazing and immersive experience that will have you backtracking to look at the main hall again and again   9 10 highly recommended whether you love resident evil or this is your first capcom game   if you re listening capcom please please please do this for dino crisis  i ll buy many copies of it     h1 gameplay    h1      for those of you who haven t followed this release closely  this remake of resident evil 2 is a reimagining of the original resident evil 2 from 1998   i use the word “reimagining” strongly because the re2 veterans will find that some areas of the game have been redesigned  or just added to lengthen the play time of the story   some enemies and areas were also removed to help players with particularly frustrating or nonsensical sections  and keep players moving through the game      with that said however  much of the game was left faithfully accurate to the original with a slight change from the resident evil 1 remake released a few years ago   while resident evil 1 changed next to nothing from the original game  resident evil 2 uses the over the shoulder camera made famous by resident evil 4   while some long time resident evil fans will find this irritating or unfaithful as a remake  i believe it allows players the ability to explore the infamous rpd from a whole new perspective  taking in the amazing visuals attainable on next generation hardware      players familiar with the over the shoulder perspective used in the 4th and 5th resident evil installments will feel comfortable with the camera in resident evil 2  as it functions much the same   enemies in this remake are a large highlight of this release  with capcom s new zombie degradation system on full display   now  shotgun shells can cut zombies clean in half  and handgun rounds can be used to slowly chip away at a zombie  removing legs  arms  and other parts to disable enemies in the best way you see fit     h1 sound    h1      the sound quality in this remake is bar setting for games to come   while i might not be able to explain how it works  resident evil 2 uses a real time binaural system  which creates some of the best and gripping sound quality i have experienced to date in a horror game   the looming sounds of mr  x will quickly create dread at every corner and hearing a licker long before you see it can put ice in your veins   if possible  i highly recommend playing with a quality pair of headphones     h1 characters   story    h1      for players returning to resident evil 2 versus players starting the story for the first time  much of the story will feel familiar   experiencing all the events of the original game in stunning modern age visuals is easily worth it   if you are new to the story of resident evil 2  you will have a great time uncovering the mysteries for the first time  from leon and claire s perspective  each with their own unique situations that arise during the game   regardless if you are new or returning  i highly recommended purchasing this game to experience the love that capcom has put into this remake     h1 replay value    h1      resident evil 2 encourages its players to go back and experience the story line multiple times from multiple perspectives and encouraging the player to do so with tangible awards   unlike many games today that set improbable goals with achievements to encourage players to go back and play the game for a second or third time to unlock these achievements  resident evil 2 brings back the much more rewarding method of older games by allowing you to unlock many different in game items that are usable in your subsequent playthroughs        after finishing the game for the first time  you will unlock the 2nd playthrough mode  which gives you the complete story from the other characters perspective   once you have beaten these modes  you can play through and try to achieve the s rank to unlock multiple different unlimited ammo weapons to use freely after they have been unlocked   in my opinion  this is what more game developers should do these days  giving players a strong motivation to complete the game again for wacky or overpowered unlockable items     h1 ui usability    h1      the user interface will feel extremely familiar to players of resident evil 7   resident evil 2 is built on the resident evil engine  which was created for resident evil 7  and not much was changed about the ui layout   while this isn t necessarily a bad thing  there is very little that has changed with inventory space  combining and examining items  or key items   the ui functions very smoothly  with no lag or hang ups and generally performs optimally   while the interface was not changed much at all  i think it lends itself to this latest resident evil well     h1 final thoughts    h1      this reimagining of resident evil 2 captures the magic of the original with all of capcom s love and creativeness in every corner of this game   the gameplay will easily hook you and have you on your third and fourth playthrough before you know it   the final score that i will give resident evil 2 is a 9 10 and only because some of the animations of enemies grabbing you can become troublesome at points  but overall can be overcome by better planning and execution   i highly recommend purchasing this for yourself  your friends  and your family so everyone you know can experience this great remake

after the series returned to its roots with re7  it s great to see capcom continuing to make actual survival horror games in the resident evil series  just think  the re2 remake could ve just been an action shootyfest if they had continued what they d been doing in re 4 6  instead  we got one of the best survival horror titles of the past couple years   i think that with this genre one of the most important aspects to cover is  is the game actually scary    and i m happy to say that re2r not only delivers  but it does so without relying on jump scares or overuse of  spooky no combat  sections  resources like ammo health pickups having genuine scarcity  foes that pose a real threat even when a room has only a single enemy present  dimly lit locales that help obscure monsters  and the body horror designs of the creatures you fight combine to form a sense of tension and terror that arises naturally from the gameplay itself    rather than being startled because a zombie grabbed you in a scripted sequence  you ll be terrified when there s two zombies in front of you and one clambering up the stairs behind you  you know you don t have enough ammo to deal with all three  and you ve only got seconds to decide on a plan before at least one of them is sinking its teeth into you  with so many  survival  horror games nowadays either providing you with more than enough resources to deal with every enemy  or going the amnesia route and making your only defense being to flee  it s wonderful to see one of the first major releases of 2019 instead managing to hit that perfect balance between the two extremes and form an experience where you have to actually  survive    moving on to the gameplay  the re engine once again delivers an incredible experience  re7 s pinpoint gun physics carry over quite well to 3rd person  with recoil having a serious impact on how well you fight if you don t make sure to manage it  and the new aim focusing system  your crosshair shrinks if you maintain your aim  adds an extra layer of strategy to the tense gunplay  enemies react differently depending on where you shoot them  if you shoot while they re attacking  or even if you shoot after knocking them over  so even before you account for the variety of strategies you can take with different guns  you have a ton of options for approaching encounters from all of the different ways you can shoot an enemy with one gun   enemies  attacks  as well as their reach or how much damage they deal  depend on what angle they re grabbing you from  if they ve been de limbed  if other enemies are close enough to join in  or etc  adding an extra element to how your spot in a room affects your survivability  the only real complaint i have about the core gameplay is that there s no  dodge  button  nor any sort of unique animations for pushing past stunned enemies  as a result  the act of weaving around a live zombie is an awkward dance of sprinting at odd angles to try to out maneuver their attacks  the sort of thing that would be a far more fluid experience if you had some sort of duck or quickstep button  you ll sometimes find yourself unable to slip past an enemy due to getting  stuck  between them and nearby world geometry even when there s a gap you should reasonably have been able to squeeze through   for as scarce as its resources are  the game offers a great deal of variation in your gear  each of the two characters has their own set of 6 guns  as well as a shared set of 3 sub weapons  from the pistols to the shotgun or grenade launcher  every gun fills its own niche in your arsenal  so your combat options genuinely expand with every new weapon you find  with no padding out due to just finding side grades  each of the character s respective loadouts have their own strengths and weaknesses  leon s shotgun is good at one shotting zombies  whereas claire s gl can stun lickers far more reliably   helping further vary your run as each character on top of their very different enemy placements   sadly  enemy variation is a tad lacking  several enemy types  various giant bugs  evolved lickers  from the original re2 aren t present  and though you still have a solid lineup with the lickers  dogs  ivys  and g adults  they re hardly if ever used in combination with each other  in every case it s zombies   another enemy  and most of those times the other enemy has a positional spawn trigger  meaning you can kill the zeds before the other enemy actually spawns  mr  x does add a nice dash of variation for his portions of the game  but beyond a single licker he once again only really mingles with the regular zeds    the boss is likewise pretty lacking  and i say  boss   because you re literally fighting the same increasingly mutated dude multiple times  his attack animations change to match his new forms  but in terms of function they remain basically the same  with maybe one new move added  leon  does  get his own final boss  but said battle also provides you with a rocket launcher halfway in that insta kills the boss   regarding balance  ammo is properly scarce  so if you go around trying to shoot every enemy in the head until they die you ll quickly find yourself out of bullets  instead  you ll have to gauge whether you can afford to expend the shots for a full kill  just go for a knockout or a de limbing  or even just risk ignoring the enemy entirely and focusing on evading them  healing is likewise limited  and on hardcore you even have limited saves  which can make a failed encounter much more debilitating if you don t plan ahead  enemies have some real durability  such that not even starting your attack from a long distance is a guarantee of safety unless you re using your stronger  and far scarce er  weapons  leading to the combat hitting that difficulty sweet spot that lends itself well to the tense survival situation the game presents   puzzles tend to be the  flip the switches in the right combination  type  leaning towards being too easy  but they at least provide a nice break in the tension  and there s an optional one that actually requires some sleuthing  if the balance is lacking anywhere in particular  it s the  2nd run  for each character  which is described as a remixed run of their story meant to better mesh plot wise with the other character s 1st run  however  this only really changes the game up until the first boss  after which it ll be identical to their 1st run bar some puzzle solutions and one ammo type being changed for another   the characters are written well enough  with nothing cringeworthy and a few scenes that actually get pretty emotional  though the personalities of minor characters tend to get a bit hammed up  seemingly in an effort to convey as much personality as possible in their very short screen times  the main plot itself is a bit ridden with holes   especially  considering 2nd run is supposed to take place alongside the other protagonist s 1st run  yet has you redoing bosses and puzzles   but as the story is mostly driven by the character interactions this won t take too much from your enjoyment   the graphics are fab  the animations fluid and varied  and the sound design lends both weight to your weapons and a real sense of threat to enemies very effectively  boss tracks are solid epic orchestral stuff  while regular areas tend to stick to ambience  or short chase loops  in the case of mr  x   which does a great job of helping the tense mood as you explore   overall  it s not perfect  but re2r is still a standup example of a solid survival horror experience  fans of the classic re style will enjoy it immensely  and it does enough different from the original that they ll be in for a fresh new experience of the story  if you re a survival horror fan in general  you re seriously missing out if you pass this one up   9 10  follow my curator to be kept up on all my reviews  including stuff i ghost write through family share so  where do i start  i ve been a huge fan of the resident evil series since i was a child  even though the games scared the hell out of me  i kept playing and i m even one of the few people who enjoy the later games  4 5 6   but going by this one  well  resident evil 2 was my favorite from the franchise along with resident evil 3  nemesis  i was skeptical about the remake when they first spoke about it being in the works  but seeing the trailers and actually playing it entirely now  it s worth every damn cent you may consider spending   for resident evil veterans  the game offers old game mechanics and mixes new mechanics in just nicely  i know what you re thinking  you re skeptical about the  over the shoulder  camera angle that was first introduced with resident evil 4  but i can tell you  it adds to the atmosphere  because you can t actually see things behind you for example  you may be focused on fighting off some enemies and another one just walks up behind you and scares you  the remake makes you remember places you re familiar with from the original resident evil 2  shows you new places and even changes the order you normally would see those places in  is that bad  by no means  is it just as good as the original  no  it s better  it looks better  plays better  has more fun mechanics and obviously  due to the lack of technological limitations the developers have nowadays  you can also expect some really surprising new stuff you would never expect in a resident evil game  is the story different  hmm   there s slight changes  but nothing drastic    for the new players  if you ve never played one of the classic resident evil games  0 1 2 3 cvx etc   then this is a good point to start in  now  i suggest you play the remake of resident evil 1 first  it s on steam as well and not that expensive  i can also wholeheartedly recommend that game  the remake is genuinely better than the ps1 original in every way possible  now back to the re2 remake  if you wanna buy this game without playing re1 first  you can absolutely do that  re2 is where the story really begins  where everything is explored in a deeper way and where actual character development happens  you may argue   resident evil 1 also had character development     yes  but not nearly as much as this  this is one of the first resident evil games that lets you feel some sort of pity or sympathy for the characters involved while the other resident evil games usually made you go  oh damn  that dude s dead   or  this guy s kinda cool   or whatever  as a hardcore resident evil fan  i promise you that this game is worth every cent and if you haven t played a resident evil game before touching this  you ll wanna play the other ones afterwards to see how the story continues  again  you ll understand everything even if you didn t play re1  but you ll understand more easter eggs and references   which add to the experience  if you played resident evil 0 or resident evil 1   surprise surprise  you can play as the rookie police officer leon s  kennedy or as claire redfield  who s looking for her brother chris  who many may remember from resident evil 1   whichever you choose to play as first  once you finished the story   and this is important  play the story of the other character too  the story is drastically different and answers all the questions that may come up during your first playthrough  i was actually surprised that it s not just a different character with different weapons  it feels like an entirely different game  even though 50  of the locations are just re used  trust me on this   tl dr  buy the game  you won t be disappointed

okay  two days in  and i ve already sunk nearly 8hrs into this game  i have been waiting for this remake for a long while  i typically never pre order games  would you believe that my last pre order was also an re game  revelations to be exact 8d   but this was a case in which i felt it was worth the risk and am glad i did  resident evil 2 introduced us to two staple characters  leon kennedy  the rookie cop who became a badass when the chips were down on his very first day  and claire redfield  the equally badass college sister of veteran chris redfield  young and inexperienced  they had to fight their way through the nightmare of a city gone to hell quite literally  it was a masterpiece  and honestly probably my absolute favorite of the original ps1 era re games   so the question remains    how does this remake hold up   as of the time of this review  i am most of the way through leon s first scenario  i may edit this review later  but i m currently unable to contain myself  p so how s that  as far as the game looks   it looks amazing  it is astounding how realistic everything looks and how the rpd and everywhere else was built so that not only are we revisiting locations we know and love  but are also experiencing them for the first time as well  by that  i mean that the world is much bigger than it was before  more realistically built  and revamped so that while you will recognize places from the original  things are still different than they were before  as for the characters  they look about as one would expect  the only major difference i feel is that leon himself looks younger than before  which honestly fits with the premise of a kid cop who just finished the academy and is both too youthful and idealistic for the nightmare that awaits  maybe that s just me  but i think the look fits him really well  gameplay   now that s a whole new ballgame  resident evil 2 constantly threw ammo at you in large quantities  enough so that it actually was feasible that you could just about destroy every t virus g virus spawn you ran across and still have a bit of ammo to spare  attempting this game with that kind of attitude will result in you being a bloody mess on the floor     yes  this game actually follows re1 s original premise regarding combat  fighting back against monsters requires you to be very careful about how you handle it  ammo is far more scarce  and while you can craft some by simple means of finding gunpowder  you will still burn through it quick if you attempt to fight every creature you see  as a result  you will have to pick and choose your battles  for those who feel that resident evil as a series has grown more action y and not very survival horror y will find that it has returned to that root with this game  be smart  find safe routes to take  and conserve ammo  or you will die  and conserve you must  as gone are the days where a pool of blood on the floor told you that a creature was dead  something is only dead when it ceases to move and doesn t move after return trips  one would think that the zombies are attempting to fake you out at this point  leaving you feeling you re safe when they fell over and yet getting back up 20min later when you thought you cleared them out  perhaps an exaggeration but point being  treat every corpse as though it s gonna get back up and don t let your guard down   okay  i m praising this a lot  so are there any bad things about it  sure  every game has a flaw of some kind  the acting can be a little iffy with me  despite leon s youth and all  he seems a tiny bit flat when facing everything  sometimes he gives a yell of surprise  but most of the time one would think this is an everyday thing for the rookie cop  also for as tense as the game gets  i don t really feel like it s ever exactly  scary   i could simply be too numb to that feeling of terror by now  but aside from the occasional scare here and there  never did the game really get me to a point that i was utterly frightened  so you may find this to be a let down if you re looking for the  thrills   chills  type gameplay  the tyrant  or mr x as many of us would call him  now becomes more of a stalker  haunting the rpd and actively looking for you from room to room  this was the closest to terror i felt  but given that it was too easy to catch his attention and him hunt me down  too many times i felt i was being chased away from my objective  desperately searching for a route to get him off my tail long enough so i could do what i had set out to  it s scary to start  but quickly can become obnoxious   again  i m not finished with the game  i may edit this review later or something once i complete leon s story and challenge the second story or claire s storylines  the game does have some downfalls with slightly sub par acting  a rather steep difficulty curve compared to the original  and a few nuances that can come off as annoying  but in the end  this is everything i could ever hope for in a remake  if you re on the fence about it  i will say that in my opinion you should get this game  it s true survival at its finest  has the nostalgia that makes me smile  and has plenty of new secrets and such to discover

rtx 2080 to reduce the stuttering to basically nonexistent   shadows high shadow cache on textures high  2gb  even though this is an 8gb gpu  ssr off external framerate cap in addition to in game 60fps limit and in game vsync  i also did something potentially dumb  using nvidia profile inspector i added the re2 exe to the driver profile for resident evil 7  since they run on the same engines and they may have per game optimizations tied to this profile   and re7 ran fine given you had sufficient vram for the texture setting you wanted to use and disabled shadow cache    the most important changes that seemed to have made the most impact is using the 2gb texture setting and adding the game to the re7 driver profile  with this the stuttering almost never happens like it did before   which was very frequent when traveling to different parts ot the station  now just once in a while or if traveling to the main hall of the station  also it may be  the more the game is played the less
