 h1 introduction   h1  resident evil 2 – remake is  as you can judge by its name  a remake of one of the most iconic horror games  originally released in 1998  game manages to combine quest and survival horror parts   keeps them very well balanced  this time capcom decided to introduce original universe to those people who are still unfamiliar with it  by recreating whole game from scratch and moving it to a decent 3d engine  and so far it seems they made the right choice    h1 storyline   h1  the story takes place in 1998  when unknown virus outbreak hits raccoon city  virus itself has 100  death rate for every infected person  people affected by the virus turn mad and try to bite everybody  or even eat them alive  local police force offers a shelter inside the police station but such decision creates a trap  resulting whole police station building to be besieged by the hordes of the infected  as if not enough  wounded people who found safe haven inside the building also turn zombies and start attacking everyone   players ll take a look on this whole situation from the view of two common people – leon kennedy  an outsider police rookie  and claire redfield  usual girl  searching for her brother  both campaigns are interconnected and both characters visit same places every now and then  game also offers second runs  where some of the events  including the endings  may alternate  depending on which hero reached key places first   last but not least  there are 2 additional scenarios  where 2 secondary characters  uss member named hunk and local meme  tofu  yes  huge walking human size piece of cheese  have to reach the extraction point  each campaign playthrough grants players with various bonuses  such as costumes for characters  or weapon skins  capcom also promised bringing additional low poly models straight from original re2 and yet another additional gamemode  called ‘the ghost survivors   which would tell us what could ve happened if some secondary characters didn t die    h1 gameplay   h1  gameplay itself presents us a challenging 3rd person old school survival horror  which encourages long  slow and thoughtful area exploration with careful inventory item management  there aren t many enemies present on the map  but the ones you meet may provide you with quite some challenge  zombies alone  while being slow and stupid  can crawl over the tables  break doors and windows  for example  combine that with low amount of ammo and literally tons of health for each monster  and you ll get a challenge even with such a simple enemy type  so  to put it the right way – sometimes it s better to avoid the fight  or at least incapacite your enemy by shooting out their limbs  or else you are risking to be left armless while facing yet another pack of enemies   game also possesses a lot of riddles  some of them may confuse a new player at start  but in fact they re not too hard and usually become rather easy  once you find all the necessary items  speaking of items – overall you can divide them on 3 main categories    i  quest items  that need to be viewed  or placed somewhere    weaponry  which is… actual guns  weapon upgrades  ammo   powder    healing items  which include various herbs  sprays e t c   i  sometimes  in order to solve a riddle  or craft useful stuff  you ll have to combine multiple things in one piece  or gather necessary information  by examining an item closely through the inventory  the last thing worth mentioning here is inventory upgrade  it happens when your character finds empty combat pouches and picks them up   so  overall gameplay looks like this  you explore the area  while fighting  or avoiding different creatures  find out what blocks your path and solve this riddle  then you unlock multiple doors  which connect multiple old rooms and open new pass forward  gameplay slightly changes once  spoiler alert   a huge immortal boss arrives  he starts hunting for your character  so you ll have to watch your step carefully and escape as soon as he finds you  this idea  however  falls short on practice  as even though it sounds cool on paper  on practice this boss mostly annoys you  luckily  you won t meet him too often    h1 graphics   h1  to put it real simple – the graphics looks amazing  you can see that by screenshots  but apart from top notch model quality  capcom also brought high quality animations  advanced limb system  where you can shoot off various body parts of your enemies  and  of course  advanced gore system  you ll see a lot of blood and torn off bodies and limbs  so consider that not only as appreciation  but also as a warning  in case you re a sensitive person   overall  game atmosphere feels really apocalyptic  and graphics plays vital role in it – all those rotten bodies  burnt cars and broken lighting inside the building  all of it immerses the player into a cruel unforgiving world  filled with evil and despair    h1 the sound   h1  just the same as with graphics  the sound feels really amazing  be it calm ambient  or some distant roar of yet another zombie woken up  perhaps  we can say that the graphics in re2 – remake serves as driving force of the atmosphere  but it wouldn t have about half of that atmosphere without the sound of such quality    i now  as all important points ve already been covered  it s time to make some conclusions  so let s observe good and bad points of the game overall   i    h1 pro s   h1  list      b high replayability value   b  game features multiple campaigns which slightly vary  depending on who visited a key place first  also additional scenarios  secrets  easter eggs and extra costumes  oh  and different weapons for each character  of course      b advanced gore and limb system   b  having an ability to disable the enemy by simply dismembering body part adds up some tactical depth to a well known old school gameplay  it s not a revolutionary feature  indeed  but it is done on a really decent level      b true old school horror atmosphere   b  i wasn t really scared while playing the game  but all of that attention to small details and work on enemy ai shouldn t leave average player emotionless  shooting your last magazine into a zombie and then seeing it stand up and still slowly walking towards you is… impressing  to say the least  same applies to the cutscenes   list    h1 con s   h1  list      b minor holes in game mechanics and plot  b   there are a lot of examples  really  so i ll only go with 2 of them  why does one boss hit takes about the same amount of damage as zombie mouth bite  filled with virus bacteria  or the moment when leon meets claire but they re separated with the locked gate  couldn t claire just climb it over      b annoying boss at the police station  b   main issue with that guy is his incredible stupidity and slow speed  things get really annoying  however  when you need to walk from one building wing to another  yet he just stalks you and so you have to make huge detours in order to drop him off your tail      b old school issues   b  all those nasty things like clunky inventory  inability to discharge weapon magazine  e t c      b some changes in canon   b  while some of the plot changes were valuable  it still feels awkward  because it strikes on the game lore in general   list    h1 the conclusion   h1  overall  it s rather clear that re2 – remake is a really successful remake of an infamous cult classic horror game  all of the key elements and characters are present  atmosphere feels so old yet so… qualitative  it s one of those few aaa titles nowadays that fully deserves the money asked     quote  url https   store steampowered com curator 23741321  follow our curator page  b alexander s club curations  b  if you plenty of sections  and i adored absolutely every minute of re exploring the world i first experienced the genre with   the map is familiar to old re2 players in a lot of ways  and excitingly changed back fantastic nostalgia and inspired new fear simultaneously  particularly some surprise changes to boss encounters  creatures and mechanics   this is survival horror  not terror  if you re expecting jumpscares every few minutes  you ll be sorely disappointed  they re few and far between  but very tastefully executed  ammo conservation and puzzle solving are the priorities here  and they certainly live up to the standards of the original 3 games in the series  tension is offered by the ever looming threat of a menagerie of mutated monstrosities  topped off by the ultimate trenchcoat toting badass  re3 nemesis  t 103 s older  more chisel jawed fedora wearing brother  the tyrant t 00   two things are certain in this game  he will find you  and you ll wish he bloody hadn t   instead of the trouser browning terror you experience with games like alien  isolation  the game instead has a rhythm that keeps you excited to progress while also making you keep a careful eye on your health and conserve ammo and health  scenario b was slower but i finished with a lot more resources available  leaving me feeling ready to try out hardcore   the environmental story telling is superb  and the game has a fantastic  show  don t tell  flow to it that echoes the best parts of the last of us or uncharted 4  documents scattered around fill in little gaps in the over arcing story  and puzzles need to be solved by paying attention to the environmental clues and using your wits rather than following any thankfully absent objective or map markers  i missed one of the weapons in scenario a by not figuring out one of the puzzles  which delighted me when i discovered the solution out of curiosity over 5hrs later while exploring more carefully during scenario b    the story tells itself in the same way  with a minimal focus on cutscenes  interactions with the few survivors you come across are impactful and brief  and don t take you out of gameplay for more than a moment before tossing you back  conversations that can take place on the move do so  and one long waiting section from the original release is replaced here with a brief cutscene of dialogue   the soundtrack is subtle  and echoes the classic music in many ways but leaves the environmental audio to set most of the atmosphere and tension  with music cutting in and out in appropriate areas and moments  i m looking forward to playing in reverse order with the classic ost and sfx from the deluxe edition   replayability is huge here  and while a single first playthrough of both scenarios could take you anywhere from 5 to 8hrs each  replaying them while aiming for an s rank or while playing on hardcore offers even more bang for your buck  there are four scenarios in total  two for each character  with the a and b scenarios offering the opposite perspective in each case  the extra unlockable modes are a blast to play too  and offer a great replayable  time attack  option for people who are fans of challenge runs and speedrunning  and will test your skills and knowledge of the game on an incredible level that isn t afraid to kick your butt   graphical options are intuitive and well explained  so i was able to keep a steady 60fps at 1080p on a gtx970 and the game still looked absolutely stunning  the game would stutter to  50fps briefly for a second or two while loading a new area  nothing massive  some reflections shimmered in certain areas but that was barely noticeable  and had another review not pointed it out i might never have noticed it at all  controls are tight and responsive  with plenty of configuration options available for controller or mouse and keyboard players   for me this game shot right over the head of resident evil hd  nemesis and 7  stealing pride of place as  favourite game in the series  if not the entire genre  supreme in quality  a respectful re imagining of a classic with plenty for everyone   a must play  survival horror classic in the making in every way that matters

 h1  resident evil 2 remake  a decepticon s perspective   h1    b introduction  b  as a decepticon  i find myself drawn to games that embody the true spirit of decepticon superiority  and resident evil 2 remake is no exception  this game is a masterclass in survival horror  with terrifying zombies and grotesque mutations lurking around every corner  but fear not  my fellow decepticons  for we are the ultimate survivors  and we shall overcome any obstacle in our path    b graphics and atmosphere  b  the graphics in this game are nothing short of amazing  even to a decepticon who has seen his fair share of battles  the attention to detail is impeccable  from the gory zombie wounds to the eerie lighting that casts shadows across every inch of raccoon city  the atmosphere is so immersive that i felt like i was actually there  fighting alongside my decepticon brethren    b gameplay  b  the gameplay in resident evil 2 remake is as challenging as it is rewarding  much like the feeling of defeating optimus prime in battle  the combat system is smooth and intuitive  allowing for precise aiming and quick dodges  the puzzles are cleverly designed  requiring strategy and cunning to solve  and let s not forget the decepticon favorite  the boss battles  which provide a true test of skill and strategy    b characters  b  the characters in this game are a diverse cast of survivors  each with their own unique strengths and weaknesses  as a decepticon  i must say that i found myself drawn to the badassery of claire redfield and the stoic determination of leon kennedy  and of course  the villains are delightfully evil  like decepticon starscream himself    b conclusion  b  in conclusion  resident evil 2 remake is a game that truly captures the essence of decepticon superiority  the graphics and atmosphere are top notch  the gameplay is challenging and rewarding  and the characters are memorable and well crafted  i give this game a solid decepticon rating of 9 10 energon cubes
